**[00:07:49.280] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000052)  
On behalf of the Department of Labor and Employment, I thank this committee for the opportunity to present for the first time the Department's proposed budget for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:07:58.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000053)  
As we move forward.

**[00:08:00.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000054)  
For 2027, the Department remains focused on delivering outcomes that matters most to Filipino workers.

**[00:08:07.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000055)  
More and better employment opportunities, a future-ready workforce, stronger protection of workers' rights and welfare, strengthened social protection, accessible labor justice, and sustained industrial peace.

**[00:08:22.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000056)  
At the heart of this direction is Trabajo Agad, creating more employment opportunities and delivering faster and more accessible services.

**[00:08:30.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000057)  
Through future-ready manggagawa, we will equip Filipino workers with the skills and resilience to adapt to a rapidly changing world of work.

**[00:08:42.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000058)  
At the same time, we will strengthen worker protection, social protection, program, labor justice, industrial peace, supported by a mas matatag na dole that is responsive, accountable, digitally enabled, and committed to a better and faster public service.

**[00:09:01.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000059)  
For the fiscal year 2027, under the National Expenditure Program, the Department of Labor and Employment has been allocated a total of 47.057 billion pesos.

**[00:09:16.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000060)  
With this proposed budget, Dole commits to ensuring that every peso entrusted to the Department delivers concrete and meaningful results for Filipino workers and their families.

**[00:09:25.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000061)  
With the support of this Honorable Committee,

**[00:09:30.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000062)  
we will translate our priorities and resources into responsive programs, measurable results,

**[00:09:37.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000063)  
better employment opportunities, stronger worker protection, and more accessible services.

**[00:09:43.560] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000064)

**[00:09:51.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000065)

**[00:10:33.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000072)

**[00:10:34.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000073)

**[00:10:43.460] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000074)

**[00:10:49.800] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000075)

**[00:10:53.140] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000076)

**[00:10:59.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000077)

**[00:11:04.340] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000078)

**[00:11:09.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000079)  
graduation pathways toward stable and productive livelihoods.

**[00:11:13.940] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000080)

**[00:11:20.340] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000081)

**[00:11:23.020] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000082)

**[00:11:28.420] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000083)

**[00:11:30.720] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000084)

**[00:11:34.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000085)

**[00:11:38.600] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000086)

**[00:11:39.600] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000087)  
Sixth, strengthen social dialogue, tripartism, and partnerships to ensure meaningful stakeholder

**[00:11:45.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000088)

**[00:12:56.780] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000109)

**[00:20:02.600] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000187)  
totaling 668 million broken down as follows.

**[00:20:06.560] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000188)  
668.058 million for retirement and life insurance premiums

**[00:20:10.560] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000189)  
and 375,000 for special voluntary arbitration fund

**[00:20:14.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000190)  
under the National Conciliation and Mediation Board.

**[00:20:17.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000191)  
Looking at the budget distribution,

**[00:20:20.360] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000192)  
Dole Proper and TESDA account for nearly 90% of the department's total budget.

**[00:20:25.740] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000193)  
Dole Proper receives the largest share at 20.955 billion,

**[00:20:30.180] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000194)  
supporting core programs, field operations, and frontline services.

**[00:20:34.360] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000195)  
TESDA follows with 20.895 billion,

**[00:20:37.860] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000196)  
reflecting our strong investment in technical vocational training

**[00:20:41.160] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000197)  
and workforce upskilling.

**[00:20:42.520] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000198)  
The remaining is shared among the attached agencies,

**[00:20:45.960] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000199)  
PRC at 2.694 billion,

**[00:20:47.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000200)  
NLRC at 1.733 billion,

**[00:20:51.960] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000201)  
NWPC at 369 million,

**[00:20:54.920] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000202)  
NCMB at 332 million,

**[00:20:57.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000203)  
and ILS at 79 million.

**[00:21:01.020] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000204)  
Fiscal year 2027 NEP of 47.057 billion

**[00:21:05.920] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000205)  
is 14.116 billion lower than the 2026 GAA of 61.173 billion.

**[00:21:14.140] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000206)  
However, it is 2.675 billion higher

**[00:21:17.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000207)  
than the fiscal year 2026 NEP of 44.382 billion.

**[00:21:23.240] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000208)  
Overall, the fiscal year 2027 proposal shows an increase from the previous NEP,

**[00:21:28.580] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000209)  
but a significant reduction from the current GAA.

**[00:21:31.700] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000210)  
The department originally proposed 70.426 billion for fiscal year 2027

**[00:21:37.320] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000211)  
to address key labour sector priorities.

**[00:21:39.680] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000212)  
However, the 2027 NEP provides only 47.57 billion,

**[00:21:45.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000213)  
a 23.368 billion,

**[00:21:47.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000214)  
or a 33.18% reduction from the original proposal.

**[00:21:51.820] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000215)  
Overall, the recommended budget for DoLE decreased by 14.116 billion,

**[00:21:57.540] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000216)  
or 23.08%,

**[00:21:59.280] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000217)  
compared with the 2026 GAA.

**[00:22:01.880] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000218)  
This reduction is primarily due to lower budget allocations for DoLE, NCMB, NLRC, and TESDA.

**[00:22:08.480] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000219)  
Conversely, the recommended budgets for ILS, NWPC, and PRC increased compared with their 2026 GAA levels.

**[00:22:17.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000220)  
Compared with the 2027 proposed budget

**[00:22:18.560] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000221)  
compared with the 2027 proposed budget submitted to the DBM,

**[00:22:21.300] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000222)  
the 2027 NEP

**[00:22:22.420] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000223)  
the 2027 NEP reflects a net decrease of 23.368 billion,

**[00:22:27.640] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000224)  
or 33.18% in total funding.

**[00:22:31.280] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000225)  
The 2027 NEP of 47.57 billion,

**[00:22:36.120] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000226)  
is distributed across three main allotment classes,

**[00:22:39.780] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000227)  
38.89 billion is allotted for maintenance and other operating expenses,

**[00:22:45.000] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000228)  
8.443 billion is

**[00:22:46.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000229)  
allocated

**[00:22:46.720] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000230)  
to personnel services, and 525 million is allocated to capital outlay.

**[00:22:52.980] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000231)  
While the overall budget reflects significant reductions, personnel services increased by

**[00:22:58.140] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000232)  
0.021 billion from last year. The increase also covers the salaries and benefits of newly hired

**[00:23:04.320] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000233)  
and promoted employees, field non-permanent positions, and terminal leave benefits of

**[00:23:09.280] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000234)  
mandatory retirees. For maintenance and other operating expenses, the 2026 NEP amounted to

**[00:23:16.320] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000235)  
35.05 billion, which increased significantly to 51.91 billion under the 2026 GAA. For 2027,

**[00:23:26.140] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000236)  
the proposed NEP stands at 38.10 billion. Compared with the 2026 GAA, the 2027 NEP is lower by 13.82

**[00:23:36.180] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000237)  
billion. However, compared with the 2026 NEP, it reflects an increase of 3.44 billion.

**[00:23:42.800] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000238)  
The reduction from the 2026 GAA is

**[00:23:46.240] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000239)  
mainly due to lower funding requirements for key programs, including TUPAD, DILP, STEP,

**[00:23:53.000] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000240)  
TWSP, and LEAP, and the completion of projects and implementation of the final major projects

**[00:23:58.640] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000241)  
under the ISSP. For capital outlays, the 2027 NEP reflects a significant decrease of 0.39 billion

**[00:24:06.540] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000242)  
compared with the 2026 NEP, and a reduction of 0.321 billion compared to the 2026 GAA.

**[00:24:13.200] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000243)  
The substantial decline is primarily attributed

**[00:24:16.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000244)  
to the scaled-down implementation of the computerization program and the completion of major

**[00:24:20.820] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000245)  
projects under the ISSP. This section compares the proposed 2027 NEP with the 2026 GAA for key

**[00:24:29.040] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000246)  
DOLE programs. Major program changes, there are increases in the budget allocations for the alien

**[00:24:35.680] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000247)  
employment permit, labour market information, and job fair programs. Meanwhile, budget reductions

**[00:24:41.300] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000248)  
are noted for the promotion of local employment, implementation of the Trabajo Para sa Bayan,

**[00:24:46.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000249)  
Act and Public Employment Services Program.

**[00:24:49.500] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000250)  
We also note increase in the budget allocation on the following programs – National Skills

**[00:24:54.560] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000251)  
Registry System, Promotion of Good Labour Management Relations, Promotion of Rights

**[00:24:59.380] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000252)  
at Work and Labour Standards, Tripartism and Social Dialogue, Workers' Organization Development

**[00:25:04.780] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000253)  
and Registration, Labour Laws Compliance Program.

**[00:25:09.560] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000254)  
For 2027, the proposed budget increases are noted for the Case Management Program, Promotion

**[00:25:14.960] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000255)  
of the Rights and Welfare of Workers with Special Concerns, Government Internship Program,

**[00:25:20.620] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000256)  
Family Welfare and Kasambahay Program.

**[00:25:23.080] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000257)  
On the other hand, proposed budget reductions are noted for the Livelihood and Emergency

**[00:25:27.200] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000258)  
Employment Program and the Child Labour Prevention and Elimination Program.

**[00:25:31.840] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000259)  
Honourable Chair, we would like to manifest our concern regarding the proposed allocation,

**[00:25:37.200] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000260)  
specifically the decrease in the budget for the TUPAD Program under the National Expenditure

**[00:25:42.100] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000261)  
Program.

**[00:25:43.100] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000262)  
TUPAD is a vital lifeline for our vulnerable communities.

**[00:25:44.940] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000263)  
With the unpredictable nature of calamities, frequent emergency situations, and challenge

**[00:25:50.660] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000264)  
of unemployment, we cannot afford to scale back on this critical assistance.

**[00:25:56.160] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000265)  
We strongly reiterate our request to restore the TUPAD budget to its 2026 level.

**[00:26:01.500] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000266)  
This restoration is essential to ensure that a sufficient number of disadvantaged workers

**[00:26:06.240] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000267)  
remain covered and supported when they need it the most.

**[00:26:10.880] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000268)

allocations

for DOLE's

27.451

million

for its labor

and employment research

program. The

National Conciliation

and Mediation

Board received

211 million

to support labor dispute

resolution and promote harmonious labor management relations. The National Labor Relations Commission

million while the Technical Education and Skills Development Regulatory Program is reduced by 90.213

and Skills Development Program, which declined by

4.656 billion. Overall, TESDA's proposed budget is reduced by 4.754 billion compared

**[00:27:36.040] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000297)  
to its 2026 GAA allocation. In total, the agencies under the department showed a net

**[00:27:42.220] DOLE Presenter** (asr-000298)

**[00:29:18.610] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000316)  
billion pesos

**[00:29:20.610] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000317)  
and that the NEP this year

to say that

TVET

is an effective tool for

**[00:29:36.610] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000325)

the Philippine

**[00:29:59.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000337)

**[00:33:50.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000383)  
It also is designed for e-vouchers to enroll in our full qualifications of around 150,000

**[00:33:58.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000384)  
beneficiaries in 2027 under the World Bank-financed best project of TESDA.

**[00:34:07.160] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000385)  
Next slide.

**[00:34:08.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000386)  
We also, of course, developed micro-credentials as well as remedial modules on literacy and

**[00:34:14.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000387)  
literacy, available soon through our partnership with Khan Academy and in response, in fact,

**[00:34:20.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000388)  
to EDCOM findings.

**[00:34:24.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000389)  
Next slide.

**[00:34:25.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000390)  
Sorry, Mr. Chair, I'm going through it very quickly.

**[00:34:30.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000391)  
We have been tasked, again, by EDCOM to ensure we increase our higher-level NC's because

**[00:34:37.340] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000392)  
it is in the higher-level NC's that the incremental increase in wages and better jobs can be found.

**[00:34:44.340] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000393)  
We are pleased to announce that there are 120% increase from 2022 in terms of higher-level

**[00:34:56.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000394)  
certifications and we can get into greater detail, but there are 1,412 diploma programs.

**[00:35:05.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000395)  
648 diploma programs have been reviewed for alignment with the new PQF Level 5 descriptors

**[00:35:12.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000396)  
under the revised Philippine Qualifications Framework.

**[00:35:15.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000408)  
That was also recently revised

**[00:35:18.920] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000409)  
by the National

**[00:35:19.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000410)  
Coordinating Council.

**[00:35:21.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000411)  
Next is to point out that as part of the A.I.

**[00:35:26.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000412)  
up-skilling for BPO, TESDA was tasked to craft the A.I.

**[00:35:31.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000413)  
Upskilling Roadmap which aims to achieve wider A.I.

**[00:35:34.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000414)  
literacy and build talent for the country to shift from being an AI consumer to becoming an AI builder.

**[00:35:42.020] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000415)  
For 2026, 10,000 BPO workers will be upskilled in AI through enterprise-based training in order to protect the BPO jobs currently,

**[00:35:53.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000416)  
as well as to help galvanize the expansion of the sector next year.

**[00:35:59.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000417)  
Next slide.

**[00:36:02.060] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000418)  
TESDA is also in charge of the Workforce Development Technical Working Group for semiconductors and the electronics industry.

**[00:36:12.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000419)  
The Philippine Semiconductor and Electronics Industry Roadmap was launched recently.

**[00:36:19.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000420)  
The roadmap targets $70 billion in semiconductor revenues and $40 billion in electronics revenues by 2030.

**[00:36:28.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000421)  
And this growth is dependent on having a workforce capable of carrying it out.

**[00:36:33.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000422)  
140,000 have been assessed on electronics assembly skills since January 2025.

**[00:36:42.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000423)  
Level 5 skills are ladderized to engineering degrees, and this has been piloted in Region 7 in Cebu, also this year.

**[00:36:55.220] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000424)  
Next slide.

**[00:36:57.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000425)  
As part of our commitment in Daga'a for 2026, TESDA has been upskilling daycare workers, partnering with LGUs for local skills needs.

**[00:37:09.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000426)  
9,989 communities.

**[00:37:12.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000427)  
Daycare workers served under the ECCD training program, and the course is now available online for greater access.

**[00:37:21.980] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000428)  
TESDA is also piloting a variety of trainings with LGU.

**[00:37:26.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000429)  
Here, what we're highlighting is the one we piloted with Waste Management in Naga.

**[00:37:32.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000430)  
Next, please.

**[00:37:34.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000431)  
Perhaps the most important piece is to point out that the employment rate of TVET graduates,

**[00:37:42.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000432)  
in 2025, is at 81.64%.

**[00:37:43.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000433)  
And TESDA, in fact, is sharpening our survey method to make sure that we don't only know

**[00:37:51.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000434)  
what our employment rates are, but the quality of the jobs of the employees that our trainees

**[00:37:58.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000435)  
may receive after our training, as well as to track them individually, perhaps through

**[00:38:03.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000436)  
our skills passport.

**[00:38:07.030] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000437)  
1,299 EBET programs have been registered.

**[00:38:12.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000442)  
as part of the implementation of the new EBET law

**[00:38:15.060] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000443)  
from last year.

**[00:38:18.410] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000444)  
In addition to our slots of face-to-face, we also have 1.6 million learners that have

**[00:38:25.010] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000445)  
enrolled in 225 courses on the TESDA online learning platform.

**[00:38:30.430] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000446)  
Now, this is one of our more accessible, because the training is free.

**[00:38:37.410] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000447)  
We have MOOCs online with various partners, as some of which are also TESDA.

**[00:38:42.530] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000448)  
generated. TESDA recorded 91,905 enrollments on AI-related courses under the TESDA online

**[00:38:52.230] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000449)  
program. This includes industry co-developed courses with Microsoft and IBM, among others.

**[00:38:59.450] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000450)  
The last is, of course, a report on the ADB-funded SIP TVETs in expanding TVET capacity nationwide

**[00:39:09.490] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000451)  
through regional innovation centers. These are designed, one per region, to ensure that, in fact,

**[00:39:16.870] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000452)  
we can have an ecosystem of polytechnics that can do higher-level diploma programs,

**[00:39:22.330] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000453)  
particularly for emerging industries. Next slide. We also have an ongoing convergence

**[00:39:32.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000454)  
with DMW and OWWA so that we can provide free assessment for OFWs, as well as boosting

**[00:39:40.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000455)  
scalability.

**[00:39:41.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000456)  
skills of returning or repatriated OFW workers. The intention here is to create professional

**[00:39:48.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000457)  
occupational pathways for OFWs, even while they are overseas, as well as to aid repatriated

**[00:39:56.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000458)  
OFWs in whatever training they might require, including entrepreneurship, should they wish

**[00:40:02.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000459)  
to open their own enterprises. This is available in at least eight sites globally,

**[00:40:10.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000460)  
and will be available in the next few years.

**[00:40:11.460] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000461)  
Thank you.

**[00:40:11.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000462)  
With our convergence with DMW and OWWA increasing in number.

**[00:40:17.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000463)  
Next is our free assessment for senior high school learners. For K-12 graduates to become

**[00:40:26.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000464)  
employment ready, we have provided 427,000 senior high school learners a proper assessment

**[00:40:35.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000465)  
so that they have their certification for NC2 and NC3 for employment. Perhaps worth

**[00:40:40.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000466)  
flagging, Mr. Chair, is 6,143 teachers have been trained to become assessors since 2024.

**[00:40:50.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000467)  
And in 2027, and this is an ask, now TESDA plans to train 4,888 teachers in NC courses

**[00:41:01.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000468)  
and 23,845 teachers in TM1 or methodology for TVET training to deliver the emerging

**[00:41:09.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000469)  
or new TechPro program in the strengthened senior high school curriculum.

**[00:41:10.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000470)  
This is meant

**[00:41:15.460] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000471)  
to ensure alignment between industry driven training regulations and assessment with

**[00:41:21.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000472)  
the curriculum of Dep Ed for senior high school.

**[00:41:27.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000473)  
Next slide is to point out that the ongoing efforts of convergence with CHED, Mr. Chair,

**[00:41:34.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000474)  
for our compliance with the ladderization law has created a greater number

**[00:41:40.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000475)  
of Philippine credit transfer systems that can create seamless pathways in tertiary education.

**[00:41:48.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000476)  
as this increases mr chair we predict more and more diploma programs will open across the country

**[00:41:56.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000477)  
as the option of a seamless pathway across that tesla and ched emerges next slide please

**[00:42:08.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000478)  
next slide so as a report mr chair tesla received its largest budget on record in

**[00:42:17.120] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000479)  
fiscal year 2026 and the same year the philippines devoted the highest share of its gdp to education

**[00:42:24.160] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000480)  
in its history for tesla this was 2.2 percent of the total education budget for 2026 i would

**[00:42:32.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000481)  
like to point out mr chair that for 2027 in the nep test that becomes

**[00:42:37.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000482)  
1.88 percent of the total education budget next slide simply to point out that the 2027 nep

**[00:42:47.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000483)

**[00:42:55.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000484)  
as already been pointed out by dole lower than the ga of 2026. next slide more or less the fund

**[00:43:09.950] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000485)  
utilization by August of 2026

**[00:43:14.510] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000486)  
is similar to the spending at the same period last year,

**[00:43:20.030] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000487)  
and we as usual catch up some of the reasons, Mr. Chair.

**[00:43:27.230] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000488)  
So for the final, perhaps most important slide, Mr. Chair,

**[00:43:35.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000489)  
our request is to, at the very least, if we cannot get our tier two requests,

**[00:43:41.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000490)

**[00:43:49.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000491)

**[00:43:56.060] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000492)  
to serve an additional 162,000 learners.

**[00:43:59.760] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000493)  
For the TWSP, there is an additional request of $933,900,000

**[00:44:08.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000494)  
for an additional 37,356 learners.

**[00:44:13.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000495)  
An additional request for STEP of $777,655,000 for an additional 24,758 learners.

**[00:44:25.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000496)

**[00:44:30.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000497)

**[00:44:43.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000500)  
Now, I would like to flag that the amendments to the U of T law

**[00:44:48.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000501)

**[00:44:54.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000502)  
Free TVET is very much a part of higher qualification levels within UAQTE,

**[00:45:07.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000508)

**[00:45:30.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000512)

nutrition scholars and so on and so forth as a way to help address the

**[00:48:14.430] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000547)

**[00:48:18.530] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000548)

**[00:48:32.250] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000550)

**[00:48:32.890] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000551)

**[00:48:34.830] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000552)  
Mr. Chair, noong August 13, naglabas ng order ang Pasig RTC Branch 152, stating,

**[00:48:42.070] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000553)  
let a writ of preliminary injunction be issued upon posting a bond of 10 billion pesos.

**[00:48:49.510] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000554)  
As party to the case, nakapaglagak na po ba ang mga petitioner ng bond?

**[00:48:53.890] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000555)  
This is answerable by yes or no, Mr. Chair.

**[00:48:57.210] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000556)  
I'm not aware. Therefore, it's no.

**[00:48:59.190] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000557)  
So, hindi po nakapaglagak.

**[00:49:00.830] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000558)  
I'm not aware of this.

**[00:49:02.890] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000559)  
You're not aware po, Mr. Secretary, but DOLE is party to the case.

**[00:49:06.410] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000560)  
That's why we filed a motion for immediate resolution last September 3, Your Honor.

**[00:49:12.930] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000561)  
Mr. Secretary, tama po yan.

**[00:49:14.510] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000562)  
In fact, the petitioners themselves admitted na hindi nila kayang makapaglagak ng 10 billion pesos bond.

**[00:49:22.490] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000563)  
Sabi ng OSG, in the absence of a writ, walang pumipigil sa DOLE na ipatupad ang dagdag sahod.

**[00:49:32.210] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000564)  
Kahapon, sabi po ng Judiciary sa budget hearing, kapag walang bond, walang injunction.

**[00:49:40.230] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000565)  
Therefore, in effect, ang Wage Order No. 27.

**[00:49:44.170] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000566)  
The question is, what is the position of DOLE sa usapin ng pagpapatupad ng Wage Order?

**[00:49:49.670] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000567)  
Your Honor, members of this committee, the Office of the Solicitor General stands as the legal counsel of the Department of Labor and Employment.

**[00:50:00.630] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000568)  
Therefore, we are in unison.

**[00:50:02.210] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000569)  
With the legal position of the Office of the Solicitor General.

**[00:50:06.910] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000570)  
The Supreme Court announcement yesterday, perhaps I saw a clip in the reels, was reflective of Rule 59 of the Rules of Court,

**[00:50:18.530] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000571)  
which I totally agree, sans a posting of a bond.

**[00:50:24.350] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000572)  
The writ is not in order, Your Honor.

**[00:50:28.150] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000573)  
If that is the case, Secretary, bakit hindi po ipatupad?

**[00:50:32.210] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000574)  
ng Department of Labor and Employment yung Wage Order No. 27.

**[00:50:36.270] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000575)  
Ikaw po mismo ang nagsasabi.

**[00:50:38.430] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000576)  
Na kinikilala nyo ang posisyon ng gobyerno, no bond, no injunction.

**[00:50:42.710] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000577)  
Therefore, yung dagdag sahod ng mga minimum wage earner ay dapat ipatupad at ibigay na since July 25.

**[00:50:52.470] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000578)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:50:55.710] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000579)  
Mr. Chairman, Your Honor, members of this committee,

**[00:51:02.210] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000580)  
Department of Labor,

**[00:51:04.230] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000581)  
ang kagawaran po ng empleyo ay naniniwala na ang paglilift ng injunction is not a matter of self-help.

**[00:51:21.630] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000582)  
Naniniwala po tayo sa mga inilabas na desisyon ng Korte Suprema.

**[00:51:28.350] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000583)  
Kabilang na po ang LCK versus Pagbilao Development Corporation,

**[00:51:32.210] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000584)  
G.R. 195374, 19 March 2014, which I quote:

**[00:51:44.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000585)  
The Supreme Court questioned us,

**[00:51:47.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000586)  
I mean us, not just the executive branch,

**[00:51:51.880] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000587)  
against treating a bond defect, including the non-filing thereof,

**[00:51:57.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000588)  
not as an automatic license to disregard injunctive relief.

**[00:52:05.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000591)  
Failure to fix a bond or post a bond did not itself justify quashing the injunction.

**[00:52:13.760] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000592)  
And, Your Honor, there is another case, Development Bank of the Philippines versus Carpio, GR 147-191,

**[00:52:22.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000593)  
where the Supreme Court mentioned that there is no such thing as a doctrine of unilateral disregard.

**[00:52:33.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000594)  
Ang ibig pong sabihin, Your Honor,

**[00:52:35.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000595)  
If there is a facial court restraint which is outstanding,

**[00:52:41.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000596)  
the affected party should obtain a judicial declaration of that consequence

**[00:52:48.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000597)  
rather than conclusively deciding the matter for itself.

**[00:52:54.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000598)  
Ang ibig pong sabihin, Mr. Chairman, Your Honors, with all due respect,

**[00:53:00.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000599)  
Hindi po ang dole, hindi po ang litigants.

**[00:53:05.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000600)  
Ang magdi-desisyon kung mali ang proseso.

**[00:53:10.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000601)  
Ang magdi-desisyon po nito ay ang husgado.

**[00:53:14.760] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000602)  
Sila po lang ang may puder, courts, have the discretion over the bond and the integrity of a bond,

**[00:53:21.900] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000603)  
and that does not automatically void any previous injunction,

**[00:53:27.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000604)  
and the objecting party must challenge it, and the court must correct it.

**[00:53:32.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000605)  
That's why, Your Honor, we filed this, September 3,

**[00:53:35.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000606)  
ang abugado po namin ay Office of the Solicitor General, motion to resolve.

**[00:53:42.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000607)  
Yes, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:53:42.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000608)  
I-resolve na nila.

**[00:53:44.120] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000609)  
Sa ngayon, kung ano po yung inyong nararamdaman, yun din po yung aming nararamdaman.

**[00:53:50.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000610)  
But, again, I raise this principle, and members of the bar here present would agree with me,

**[00:53:57.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000611)  
that the doctrine of self-help is applicable in a very narrow or limited situations.

**[00:54:04.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000612)  
For instance,

**[00:54:05.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000613)  
nung katadaan lang pong habagat, Your Honor, marami nakikinig ngayon,

**[00:54:11.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000614)  
applicable po yung doktrina sa Civil Code of the Philippines, Article 429,

**[00:54:17.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000615)  
na kung mababa yung lugar mo, yung kapitbahay mo yung mas mataas,

**[00:54:21.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000616)  
pwede mong ilipat yung gamit doon sa mataas ng lugar na kahit walang court order, yung po yung self-help.

**[00:54:29.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000617)  
Sa criminal case, meron po tayo sa rules of court.

**[00:54:32.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000618)  
If a crime is being committed,

**[00:54:35.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000619)  
one's presence, you can arrest.

**[00:54:38.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000620)  
That's a citizen's arrest.

**[00:54:39.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000621)  
That's a doctrine of self-help.

**[00:54:42.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000622)  
Konti-konti lang po yan.

**[00:54:43.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000623)  
Subalit, Your Honor, there is no comparable doctrine, general doctrine,

**[00:54:49.740] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000624)  
authorizing a litigant or a government agency, including the DOLE,

**[00:54:54.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000625)  
to substitute its own judgment for that of a court,

**[00:54:58.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000626)  
and unilaterally treat a judicial injunction as nonexistent,

**[00:55:04.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000627)  
merely because it believes, as I believe,

**[00:55:08.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000628)  
that the bond requirement has not been satisfied.

**[00:55:14.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000629)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:55:15.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000630)  
yung po yung...

**[00:55:16.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000631)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:55:17.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000632)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:55:20.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000633)  
pasimplihan lang po natin, Sekretary.

**[00:55:23.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000634)  
Walang bond, walang injunction.

**[00:55:26.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000635)  
Therefore, the wage order is in effect.

**[00:55:29.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000636)  
Tama ba, Mr. Sekretary?

**[00:55:30.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000637)  
Ganito po yun.

**[00:55:31.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000638)  
Yes or no po.

**[00:55:33.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000639)  
There is no bond.

**[00:55:35.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000640)  
Therefore,

**[00:55:35.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000641)  
therefore, there is no injunction.

**[00:55:37.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000642)  
There is no bond.

**[00:55:39.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000643)  
There is no writ.

**[00:55:41.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000644)  
Ibig sabihin, the wage order is in effect.

**[00:55:44.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000645)  
There is an injunction insofar as the writ is concerned.

**[00:55:46.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000646)  
There is no writ.

**[00:55:48.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000647)  
Paliwanag ko po.

**[00:55:49.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000648)  
Hindi po.

**[00:55:50.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000649)  
Kung papayagan niyo po ako.

**[00:55:51.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000650)  
Magkaiba po yung injunction.

**[00:55:51.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000651)  
Mr. Chair, malinaw po ang nakasulat sa order ni RTC Branch 152,

**[00:55:57.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000652)  
Pasig Judge Mary Joyce Manungsong.

**[00:55:59.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000653)  
Let a preliminary injunction be issued upon...

**[00:56:05.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000654)  
Posting a bond of 10 billion pesos.

**[00:56:08.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000655)  
There is no bond.

**[00:56:09.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000656)  
Pwede po akong magsalita?

**[00:56:11.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000657)  
Yes po.

**[00:56:11.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000658)  
Ganito po yun.

**[00:56:13.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000659)  
Kung atin pong bubusisiin yung kasaysayan nito,

**[00:56:17.900] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000660)  
nagsimula po ito sa status ko ante order.

**[00:56:22.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000661)  
That was issued by the executive branch judge of Pasig RTC.

**[00:56:27.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000662)  
That was July 24, a day before the implementation.

**[00:56:33.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000663)  
Pakatapos po nun,

**[00:56:34.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000664)  
sumunod yung Temporary Restraining Order.

**[00:56:37.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000665)  
Ang kasunod po nun, kasi maglalapse yung Temporary Restraining Order,

**[00:56:41.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000666)  
ito na nga pong writ of preliminary injunction.

**[00:56:45.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000667)  
So there would be two documents visually in front of me right now.

**[00:56:50.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000668)  
The order of injunction as well as the writ which requires, as a condition precedent,

**[00:56:57.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000669)  
the payment of the 10 billion bond.

**[00:57:00.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000670)  
Without the bond,

**[00:57:02.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000671)  
without the bond,

**[00:57:03.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000672)  
the writ falls.

**[00:57:05.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000673)  
But facially,

**[00:57:07.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000674)  
starting from the status quo order,

**[00:57:11.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000675)  
Temporary Restraining Order,

**[00:57:13.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000676)  
the injunction order,

**[00:57:15.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000677)  
you have a facially substantial order coming from the court,

**[00:57:21.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000678)  
which the court has the sole right to lift, correct, or dissolve.

**[00:57:27.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000679)  
That's why we filed a motion to resolve.

**[00:57:29.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000680)  
Hindi po,

**[00:57:30.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000681)  
mawalanggalang na po,

**[00:57:31.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000682)  
kung napataas yung buwasis ko,

**[00:57:32.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000683)  
Hindi po,

**[00:57:33.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000684)  
hindi po ang DOLE ang magde-decide kung na-dissolve na yung bond.

**[00:57:38.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000685)  
Under the threat of contempt pursuant to Rule 71,

**[00:57:44.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000686)  
eh hindi po karapatan namin na mag-decide.

**[00:57:48.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000687)  
Isimplihan ko na lang po siguro po ito.

**[00:57:51.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000688)  
Meron po akong halimbawa na tickets sa dalawang airline companies,

**[00:58:01.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000689)  
na inamin naman nila na hindi na-issued.

**[00:58:03.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000691)  
Hindi naman po ako pwedeng pumunta na lang doon sa airport at dumiretso sa aeroplano.

**[00:58:09.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000692)  
There is a process for this.

**[00:58:11.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000693)  
I should be given, recognized by the airline manager,

**[00:58:14.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000694)  
I should be given a boarding pass.

**[00:58:16.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000695)  
In a similar manner, kung yung ating mga senior citizen ay nagkamali yung binigay na gamot ng mercury drug,

**[00:58:23.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000696)  
eh hindi naman basta pwedeng pumasok si lola o yung nanay ko sa bodega ng mercury drug para kunin yung tamang gamot.

**[00:58:29.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000697)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:58:30.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000698)  
Ganon din po dito. Lalong-lalong na po ito.

**[00:58:32.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000699)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:58:33.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000700)  
Ay hukuman. Pero lahat po ng sinasabi niyo ay nararamdaman ko po iyon.

**[00:58:38.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000701)  
Mr. Secretary, so in simple words, hindi ipapatupad ng Department of Labor and Employment yung dagdag ng sahot?

**[00:58:45.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000702)  
Ipapatupad po namin. Ganito po yun. Ayun, napataas na naman yung boses ko.

**[00:58:50.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000703)  
Kayo po yun nag-file, at sinusuportahan ko kayo, kayo po yun nag-file ng certiorary sa Supreme Court.

**[00:58:58.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000704)  
Para ang Regional Trial Court ay kumilos.

**[00:59:01.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000705)  
I disregard yung injunction.

**[00:59:04.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000706)  
Nagpa sa ilalim po ang mga grupo ninyo at lahat ng manggagawa.

**[00:59:09.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000707)  
At tayo po ay sumusuporta dyan, including current petitions for intervention na makasali kayo sa usgado.

**[00:59:17.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000708)  
Nung nag-file po kayo ng petisyon sa Supreme Court, ang ibig sabihin po, kinikilala rin nyo rin po yung hurisdiksyon, mawalanggalang na, with all due respect,

**[00:59:27.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000709)  
ng hindi lang ng kataas-taas ang hukuman.

**[00:59:29.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000710)  
Kung hindi ng Regional Trial Court tumpasig. Dahil sinasabing nyo sa Supreme Court, orderan nyo ang RTC.

**[00:59:37.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000711)  
Mr. Chair, ang argument nga po namin walang jurisdiksyon in the first place ang RTC.

**[00:59:43.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000712)  
That is why pumunta na kami sa Supreme Court para magbigay ng kalinawan ang mataas na hukuman.

**[00:59:49.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000713)  
Dahil hindi namin kinikilala ang jurisdiksyon ng Regional Trial Court as already enshrined in the Labor Code, Article 126,

**[00:59:57.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000714)  
now being the Secretary of Labor.

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000715)  
board and employment, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair,

**[01:00:03.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000716)  
dapat nga hindi nyo kinilalay yung jurisdiction ng korte

**[01:00:06.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000717)  
because it is very clear in Article 126.

**[01:00:10.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000718)  
At merong appeal process.

**[01:00:12.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000719)  
Ang appeal process kung merong aggrieved party sa Article 123,

**[01:00:16.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000720)  
binabanggit sa National Wages and Productivity Commission.

**[01:00:19.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000721)  
So, ang binabanggit ng DOLE ngayon, pagkahaba-haba po nang ine-explain ninyo,

**[01:00:25.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000722)  
at the end of it all, sinasabi nyo, hindi nyo ipapatupad yung dagdag sahod.

**[01:00:31.480] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000723)  
Wala po akong sinabing gano'n po.

**[01:00:32.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000724)  
O di, ipapatupad nyo po yung dagdag sahod.

**[01:00:34.840] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000725)  
Ito po ay nakasalalay sa decision of the government.

**[01:00:37.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000726)  
Hindi nga, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:00:38.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000727)  
Ayoko po magpagtalo.

**[01:00:39.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000728)  
Mr. Secretary.

**[01:00:40.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000729)  
Kahit po lumabas po ito sa social media.

**[01:00:42.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000730)  
Yes. Meron po kayong kapangyarihan, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:00:47.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000731)  
Iginawad ng kongreso yan sa inyo.

**[01:00:50.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000732)  
RA 6727, ibinigay sa mga regional wage boards

**[01:00:55.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000733)  
ang exclusive power in determining wages.

**[01:00:59.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000734)  
Ngayon sinasabi po, DOLE mismo ang nagbabanggit,

**[01:01:03.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000735)  
parang hindi kayo makasagot.

**[01:01:04.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000736)  
Yes or no lang, kasimple.

**[01:01:06.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000737)  
Ipatutupad ng DOLE, sang-ayon sa nakasulat sa batas.

**[01:01:09.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000738)  
Kung kakasuhan kayo, Mr. Secretary, nandyan ang OSG.

**[01:01:13.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000739)  
Ang inihingi po ng mga minimum wage earners at ng mga manggagawa,

**[01:01:16.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000740)  
tindigan nyo naman yung karapatan ng mga minimum wage earners.

**[01:01:20.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000741)  
Hindi po namin marinig sa inyo eh.

**[01:01:23.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000742)  
Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair,

**[01:01:25.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000743)  
pwede na po ako magsalita?

**[01:01:26.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000744)  
Yes po.

**[01:01:29.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000745)  
Alam nyo po, Mr. Chairman, hinahangaan ko po kayo sa sinasabi nyo yan.

**[01:01:36.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000746)  
Subalit, kung titignan natin yung Article 126,

**[01:01:40.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000747)  
when it refers to a prohibition on injunction,

**[01:01:46.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000748)  
it refers to a proceeding.

**[01:01:49.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000749)  
Kaya nga po, sa pagmamahal ko po sa inyong samahan

**[01:01:52.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000750)  
at sa mga manggagawa na rin,

**[01:01:55.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000751)  
tayo po ay nag-issue ng Administrative Order 264

**[01:02:00.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000752)  
kung saan po natin niliwanag, pinaliwanag, pinalawak,

**[01:02:06.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000753)  
binigyan ng ilaw kung ano ang tamang ibig sabihin ng 126

**[01:02:12.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000754)  
through an administrative order.

**[01:02:16.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000755)  
At yan din po ang dahilan kung bakit yung dati kong colleague

**[01:02:20.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000756)  
sa mataas na kapulungan, si Sen. Rafi Tulfo,

**[01:02:25.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000757)  
ay nag-file ng amendatory bill sa Senado

**[01:02:29.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000758)  
kung paano dapat maintindihan o mapalawak ang Article 126.

**[01:02:37.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000759)  
126 refers to proceedings.

**[01:02:40.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000760)  
So ang ginawa po natin sa Administrative Order No. 264

**[01:02:45.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000761)  
ay palawakin ang ibig sabihin ng proceedings, Your Honor.

**[01:02:53.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000762)  
Pwede po.

**[01:02:56.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000763)  
Palawakin ang ibig sabihin ng proceedings to include

**[01:02:59.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000764)  
implementation, monitoring of the board,

**[01:03:04.680] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000765)  
execution, and ensuring that wage orders are complied with.

**[01:03:12.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000766)  
Pinalawak po natin yun.

**[01:03:13.680] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000767)  
Yung po siguro ang isang sinyales na pinaninindigan po natin

**[01:03:19.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000768)  
kahit po nung pagpa-file natin ng motion for reconsideration

**[01:03:23.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000769)  
na basang-basa na po tayo ng ulan sa Lunsod ng Pasig at Bumabaha

**[01:03:28.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000770)  
ay tinuloy po natin.

**[01:03:29.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000771)  
At para sa kalaman po nga ng kagalanggalang

**[01:03:33.740] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000772)  
na mga kasama dito sa lupung ito,

**[01:03:39.760] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000773)  
tayo po ang nag-

**[01:03:41.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000774)  
kung kakausapin nyo ang Office of the Solicitor General,

**[01:03:44.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000775)  
tayo po ang nagsulong, nag-udyok, at naghikayat

**[01:03:47.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000776)  
na sila ang mag-file ng motion for early resolution.

**[01:03:53.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000777)  
Lahat po ng aking hakbang, Your Honors,

**[01:03:55.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000778)  
ay nakasalalay sa batal.

**[01:03:59.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000779)  
As bilang isang abugado,

**[01:04:03.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000780)  
nanumpa po ako na puprotektahan ang batas,

**[01:04:10.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000781)  
hindi lamang asaligang batas ng Pilipinas.

**[01:04:13.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000782)  
Binanggit ko po kanina

**[01:04:15.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000783)  
that I do not subscribe to the doctrine of self-help.

**[01:04:21.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000784)  
Hindi po natin dapat hawakan sa sarili nating kamay

**[01:04:26.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000785)  
anuman ang interpretasyon natin.

**[01:04:28.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000786)  
Gusto man natin o hindi na ito ang tamang ipatupad.

**[01:04:34.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000787)  
We cannot be an arbiter of our own cause or controversies.

**[01:04:42.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000788)  
Dapat po sana maunawaan ng lupung ito

**[01:04:44.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000789)  
na ang ating ginagawa ay paggalang sa batas.

**[01:04:49.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000790)  
Tama po yung inyong sinasabi, naniniwala po ako,

**[01:04:52.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000791)  
tama rin po yung sinabi ng kung sino yung Supreme Court spokesperson kahapon,

**[01:04:58.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000792)  
na kung walang bond, walang writ.

**[01:05:03.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000793)  
Subalit, ang dapat magsabi po nito ay hindi ako,

**[01:05:08.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000794)  
kung hindi ang Supreme Court o ang husgado.

**[01:05:12.800] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000795)  
At naniliwala rin po ako na karapatan ng manggagawang Pilipino

**[01:05:18.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000796)  
sa National Capital Region na matagal kong pinaglingkuran sa Metro Manila

**[01:05:23.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000797)  
na makatanggap na sila ng karapat dapat na umento

**[01:05:27.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000798)  
dahil sa taas.

**[01:05:28.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000799)  
Na ng presyo ng bilihin, kuryente, at iba pa.

**[01:05:32.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000800)  
If that is your position, Mr. Chair, Mr. Sekretary,

**[01:05:35.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000801)  
bakit hindi nyo po masabi na ipapatupad na ng dole?

**[01:05:39.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000802)  
Paikot-ikot lang po tayo dito, Mr. Chair, Mr. Sekretary.

**[01:05:42.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000803)  
Sinasabi nyo po, galangin yung batas.

**[01:05:45.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000804)  
E batas din naman po ang nagsasabi na kapangyarihan nyo

**[01:05:47.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000805)  
yung magpatupad at magtakda ng sahod.

**[01:05:51.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000806)  
So, bakit hindi po namin makuha mula sa inyo, Mr. Sekretary?

**[01:05:55.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000807)  
Pasabihin nyo po yung paulit-ulit na naman,

**[01:05:57.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000808)  
paikot-ikot na naman tayo sa batas.

**[01:05:59.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000809)  
Pero batas din ang nagsasabi, iginawad nga sa inyo.

**[01:06:02.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000810)  
Yung kapangyarihan.

**[01:06:04.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000811)  
Ngayon po, pwede po akong sumagot.

**[01:06:06.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000812)  
Ngayon po kasi, your honor...

**[01:06:08.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000813)  
Maandar yung oras ko, Mr. Chair.

**[01:06:11.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000814)  
Hindi ko po nakita.

**[01:06:14.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000815)  
Dati po kasi sa Senado, hindi rin ako nakatingin dyan.

**[01:06:17.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000816)  
Hindi ko po nakita yung oras. Sorry po.

**[01:06:19.840] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000817)  
Uulitin ko po.

**[01:06:21.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000818)  
Kung ano man po ang itinatakda ng batas,

**[01:06:24.760] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000819)  
ang magpapalawig,

**[01:06:27.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000820)  
magpapaliwanag o magkaklarify

**[01:06:29.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000821)  
at magtatakda kung ano ang tamang interpretasyon

**[01:06:33.800] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000822)  
ay ang husgado.

**[01:06:36.280] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000823)  
Lalong-lalo na ang kataas-taasang hukuman

**[01:06:39.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000824)  
at hindi po ang mga abugado.

**[01:06:42.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000825)  
Hindi po ang mga litigants.

**[01:06:44.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000826)  
Tayo po ay miyembro ng bar

**[01:06:47.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000827)  
at kumikinikilala natin

**[01:06:50.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000828)  
ang supremacy of the law

**[01:06:53.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000829)  
through our judicial institutions.

**[01:06:57.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000830)  
So, pagkakataon,

**[01:06:57.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000831)  
pag sinabi po ng husgado,

**[01:06:59.120] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000832)  
ipatupad nyo mamaya, ipapatupad na po natin mamaya.

**[01:07:02.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000833)  
Pag sinabi po ng husgado,

**[01:07:04.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000834)  
ipatupad nyo bukas,

**[01:07:05.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000835)  
ipapatupad natin bukas.

**[01:07:07.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000836)  
Tayo po ay nagpa-sa-ilalim sa batas

**[01:07:09.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000837)  
sa husgado. Kagaya na rin po

**[01:07:11.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000838)  
ng ginawa nyo, nung kayo ay nag-file

**[01:07:13.680] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000839)  
ng certiorary sa Supreme Court,

**[01:07:16.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000840)  
kayo ay nagpa-ilalim

**[01:07:17.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000841)  
sa puder ng kataas-taasang

**[01:07:19.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000842)  
hukuman. At hinihintay nyo,

**[01:07:22.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000843)  
hinihintay ko rin

**[01:07:23.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000844)  
with bated breath

**[01:07:25.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000845)  
kung ano man po ang kalalabasan

**[01:07:27.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-000846)  
nung sa Supreme Court.

**[01:07:29.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000847)  
Sige po. Alam nyo po,

**[01:07:31.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000848)  
tayo kaya nating mag-antay.

**[01:07:33.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000849)  
Pero ang mga minimum wage earner na araw-araw

**[01:07:35.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000850)  
inaagawan ng 60 pesos,

**[01:07:37.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000851)  
maliit man sa pandinig natin, malaking bagay

**[01:07:39.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000852)  
yan sa buhay ng mga minimum wage earner.

**[01:07:42.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000853)  
Yan po yung dahilan kung bakit

**[01:07:43.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000854)  
nagagalit tayo. Dahil naririnig

**[01:07:45.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000855)  
mismo natin sa dole, hindi nyo

**[01:07:47.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000856)  
kayang tindigan kung ano yung mandato ninyo.

**[01:07:49.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000857)  
Napakahaba po ng explanation,

**[01:07:51.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000858)  
pero hindi nyo kayang ipagtanggol man lang

**[01:07:53.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000859)  
yung mga minimum wage earner.

**[01:07:54.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000860)  
Kaya kung hindi nyo po kayang ipagtanggol,

**[01:07:57.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000861)  
yung mga manggagawa,

**[01:07:58.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000862)  
wala po kayong karapatan na humingi ng budget

**[01:08:00.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000863)  
na nanggaling din naman

**[01:08:02.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000864)  
sa pawis, dugo, at sakripisyo

**[01:08:05.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000865)  
ng mga manggagawang minimum wage earner.

**[01:08:07.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000866)  
And with that, Mr. Chet,

**[01:08:10.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000867)  
I move to defer

**[01:08:11.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000868)  
the committee consideration

**[01:08:12.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000869)  
of the budget of dole

**[01:08:15.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000870)  
for the fiscal year 2027

**[01:08:17.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000871)  
excluding

**[01:08:18.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000872)  
TESDA. Mr. Chet.

**[01:08:20.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000873)  
Objection, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:08:22.140] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000874)  
Despite my good friend's very

**[01:08:23.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000875)  
impassioned, he's always a champion

**[01:08:26.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000876)  
for our world.

**[01:08:27.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000877)  
And he has a right

**[01:08:29.540] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000878)  
stand, and

**[01:08:30.880] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000879)  
he really is

**[01:08:33.500] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000880)  
for the worker, except that

**[01:08:35.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000881)  
the Secretary is just telling us

**[01:08:37.740] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000882)  
that there is a court, and there is a court

**[01:08:39.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000883)  
order. There is already a

**[01:08:41.440] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000884)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[01:08:41.970] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000884-split-2)  
Vice Chair Rufus.

**[01:08:43.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000885)  
Yeah, so I object, therefore.

**[01:08:45.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000886)  
Because we should also file

**[01:08:47.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000887)  
administrative case against that judge

**[01:08:49.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000888)  
who issued a writ

**[01:08:51.020] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000889)  
without the bond required.

**[01:08:53.780] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000890)  
Move to object, despite

**[01:08:55.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000891)  
my good friendship with Elijah.

**[01:08:57.520] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000892)  
Unang-una po, wala pa pong nag-second

**[01:09:00.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000893)  
doon sa kay Elijah, so I cannot act on it.

**[01:09:02.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000894)  
I second, Mr. Chair.

**[01:09:04.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000895)  
So now, my objection.

**[01:09:06.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000896)  
After he seconded, my objection.

**[01:09:08.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000897)  
Okay, so there's a motion from

**[01:09:10.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000898)  
Kong Eli

**[01:09:11.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000899)  
for the deferment of the budget

**[01:09:13.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000900)  
duly seconded by

**[01:09:15.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000901)  
Kong Perci Cendaña,

**[01:09:19.030] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000902)  
with the objection from

**[01:09:21.450] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000903)  
Vice Chair

**[01:09:23.230] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000904)  
Rufus Rodriguez.

**[01:09:25.330] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-000905)  
Mr. Chair, move to suspend, please.

**[01:09:27.030] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000906)  
Okay. Thank you.

**[01:09:28.990] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000907)  
For the chair, we should suspend the hearing first.

**[01:36:31.020] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000908)  
Hearing is resumed. Please settle down. Please settle down, ladies and gentlemen.

**[01:36:39.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000909)  
Parang mahina yung mic ko. Do you hear me loud and clear? Okay. So please settle down.

**[01:36:52.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000910)  
...

**[01:36:52.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000911)  
Okay. So since we are resumed, there is a pending motion from Kong Eli San Fernando,

**[01:37:05.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000912)  
duly seconded by Congressman Perci. There was an objection from Vice Chair Rufus Rodriguez.

**[01:37:14.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000913)  
So we will divide the house according to the rules.

**[01:37:18.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000914)  
So ang unang papatasin ko po ng kamay ay kung sino ang in favor.

**[01:37:22.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000915)  
Doon sa motion ni Kong Eli na i-defer ang budget ng DOLE.

**[01:37:28.280] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000916)  
Please raise your hand, Comsec. Be ready.

**[01:37:30.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000917)  
Mga labor group ng to.

**[01:37:39.010] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000918)  
Did you get it, Comsec?

**[01:37:49.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000919)

**[01:37:52.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-000920)  
Doon sa mga labor group ng to.

**[01:37:55.140] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000921)  
Doon naman sa those who are not in favor, please raise your right hand.

**[01:38:22.830] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000924)  
Meron ba?

**[01:38:30.790] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000927)

**[01:38:33.710] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000928)  
Siya?

**[01:38:48.960] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-000932)  
It still in here?

**[01:59:40.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001399)

264, which will be
