prospective

**[01:59:58.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001408)  
in application.

**[02:00:00.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001409)  
Para nang sa ganun po ay magabayan yung ating mga regional wage boards sa mga susunod na pagkakataon na kanilang ipatutupad yung bagong wage orders.

**[02:00:13.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001410)  
At kabilang po dito ang nakaambana sa Region 7, sa Calabar Zone, at sa Region 3.

**[02:00:22.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001411)  
So yung Administrative Order 264 clarified prospectively.

**[02:00:27.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001412)  
What they should do, including monitoring, execution, and implementation.

**[02:00:37.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001413)  
Naniniwala po kami that with this order ay mangangambana na po siguro yung iba namang magfafile.

**[02:00:44.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001414)  
Pero hindi pa rin siguro natin masasawat na lahat.

**[02:00:48.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001415)  
Pero dahil doon sa ating inilabas at kagaya po nung sinabi ko si Sen. Tulfo ay meron ding finile sa mataas na kapulungan,

**[02:00:57.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001416)  
ay palagay ko po ito ay magiging sapat na balakid para hindi sila magsulong free-willingly ng mga ganoong court cases, Your Honor.

**[02:01:11.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001417)  
Mr. Chair, just for the record, so pag lumabas po ang mga wage order na ito na pending sa iba't ibang regions,

**[02:01:17.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001418)  
paninindigan po ba ito ng DOLE kahit na may lumabas pa na injunction from other courts in these regions?

**[02:01:24.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001419)  
Paninindigan po natin yan dahil klaro na po ngayon.

**[02:01:27.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001420)  
Yung with Administrative Order 264, in-expand na po natin yung definition ng proceedings.

**[02:01:34.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001421)  
Sa Article 126, yung proceedings ay yung proceedings, yung pagpulong sa isang hapag ng isang wage board o ng kakomisyon ng NWPC.

**[02:01:46.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001422)  
So balit dito ay maliwanag na kung ano yung mga gagawin.

**[02:01:49.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001423)  
So sa paninindigan, dati na po naman natin pinanindigan.

**[02:01:54.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001424)  
So balit ngayon mas matibay po yung pinangahawakan natin.

**[02:01:57.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001425)  
ng mga regional wage boards, pinangahawakan nila na dapat talagang ipatupad.

**[02:02:03.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001426)  
At yan po ang abangan at babantayan po ng ating mga manggagawa.

**[02:02:06.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001427)  
Kamakailan lamang po, nakailang beses akong bumalik doon sa picket line ng DUWU, yung DHL United Workers Union, na nandyan po sa sukat.

**[02:02:15.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001428)  
At sumulat din po ako sa inyong tanggapan at just yesterday natanggap ko po yung inyong tugon.

**[02:02:21.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001429)  
At gusto ko lang pong linawin kasi ang sinasabi ng DHL management ay nag-terminate daw po ng kontra,

**[02:02:27.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001430)  
ng Robinsons Supermarket Corporation.

**[02:02:29.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001431)  
Kaya bigla nilang isasarado yung kanila pong warehouse sa sukat dahil ito daw po ay wala ng reason para mag-open.

**[02:02:37.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001432)  
So ito po ay malinaw na union busting.

**[02:02:39.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001433)  
Ang tanong ko po sa Dole, nakita nyo po ba yung termination na letter or agreement between DHL and Robinsons Supermarket Corporation?

**[02:02:51.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001434)  
Nakatutok po tayo dyan yung ating pong NCMB at yung ilan po nating,

**[02:02:57.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001435)  
Assistant Secretaries.

**[02:02:58.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001436)  
In fact, merong mga pangkasalukuyang pag-uusap at alam ko naman po, Your Honor, kayo nakatutok din.

**[02:03:04.680] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001437)  
Siguro ipagpaumanhinan na nahuli ng dating yung aking tugon dahil napakaraming mga holiday noong mga nakaraang araw dahil sa habagat.

**[02:03:14.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001438)  
Pero kung makukuha niyo po ang detalye, nandito si Asec Kati sa akin.

**[02:03:22.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001439)  
Hindi ko alam kung saan siya nakatayo.

**[02:03:24.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001440)  
Si Asek Kati, can you provide some details?

**[02:03:27.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001441)  
As to depending DHL concern without sacrificing some quasi-subjudice materials that would impact the current conciliation.

**[02:03:46.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001442)  
Go ahead, Asek Kati.

**[02:03:50.000] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001443)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honors, good afternoon po.

**[02:03:54.540] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001444)  
To answer your question, with permission of the Secretary, ang nakita lang po namin ay yung service agreement na huli. Yun po yung nakita ng Department of Labor for the first five years.

**[02:04:11.840] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001445)  
Ang NCMB po ang nakaka-

**[02:04:13.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001446)  
Can you repeat that, please? Medyo mahina po. If you can speak closer to the microphone. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:04:17.760] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001447)  
Yes, sir. Through the NCMB, Your Honor, nakita po namin yung service agreement which…

**[02:04:24.540] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001448)  
For the first five years. 2018 to 2025 po ang expiry niya.

**[02:04:30.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001449)  
Opo. Kasi po, kung matatanda po ng ating mga kababayan, ang mismo pong DHL, in-announce last year na na-extend for another three years yung kanila pong kontrata sa DHL.

**[02:04:40.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001450)  
Nakita po ba natin ito, yung pronouncement na ito, mismo ng DHL?

**[02:04:44.920] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001451)  
Hindi po, Your Honor.

**[02:04:46.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001452)  
Marami po kasi nagtatanong, why is the DOLE giving credence to this union buster that is DHL?

**[02:04:53.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001453)  
Kaya po, yung isa po sa tanong.

**[02:04:54.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001454)  
Kung natin sa DOLE, ay bakit hindi niyo po ginamit ang Article 292B ng Labor Code para pigilan ang mass termination na ating mga gagawa sa DHL po?

**[02:05:06.120] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001455)  
Sir, kasi po, at this point po, nakasara na po ang DHL. Na-terminate na po yung service agreement and nagkaroon na po ng transition.

**[02:05:17.160] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001456)  
So, we, on the part of the Department of Labor, it has become moot na po.

**[02:05:24.540] DOLE OIC-Assistant Secretary Catherine L. Parado** (asr-001457)  
To suspend any termination since the termination of the employee through redundancy, based on redundancy, took effect August 31 of this year.

**[02:05:39.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001458)  
Nakababahala po, Mr. Chair, na isa itong malinaw na union busting.

**[02:05:42.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001459)  
Kaya po, marami nagtatanong, ano ang mekanismo natin para magpigilan na ganitong labor rights violation sa ating bansa?

**[02:05:49.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001460)  
Para pong ganun-ganun na lang, kadali para sa isang malaking kumpanya tulad ng DHL.

**[02:05:53.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001461)  
Natapakan ang karapatanan.

**[02:05:54.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001462)  
But let me move on to another issue.

**[02:05:58.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001463)  
Puta naman po tayo sa issue ng NEA.

**[02:06:00.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001464)  
We appreciate DOLE for establishing the Electric Cooperative Industry Tripartite Council.

**[02:06:06.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001465)  
Dahil matagal na itong demand ng labor movement.

**[02:06:09.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001466)  
Ngunit concerned pa rin po tayo, dahil hanggang sa ngayon, NEA merely suspended rather than rescinded the policies that allow it to interfere in collective bargaining.

**[02:06:20.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001467)  
Can we get the commitment of the good secretary to issue a joint issuance?

**[02:06:23.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001468)  
Upholding workers' rights to organize, bargain collectively, and strike even before the ECITC is fully operational?

**[02:06:36.760] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001469)  
May kinakausap kasi.

**[02:06:39.800] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001470)  
ako ni Congressman Vergara,

**[02:06:41.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001471)  
But I can respond. I can respond.

**[02:06:42.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-001472)  
Okay, go ahead.

**[02:06:43.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001473)  
Alam niyo po, Your Honor, for the first time, we sought the complete attendance of National Electrification Administration,

**[02:06:52.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001474)  
na si Administrator Nani Almeda, not just because of this pending concern,

**[02:06:58.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001475)  
but because we are aware that we were flagged by the International Labor Organization likewise.

**[02:07:02.120] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001476)  
The administration of NEA is now actively pursuing a tripartite system likewise within the sector.

**[02:07:14.680] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001477)  
We understood, we took note of their concerns.

**[02:07:18.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001478)  
Ang sinasabi po nila, meron daw po mga ilang electric cooperatives na talagang malaki ang kinikita.

**[02:07:26.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001479)  
Meron naman na...

**[02:07:28.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001480)  
Maliliit.

**[02:07:28.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001481)  
Meron namang electric cooperatives na yung management took advantage pretending themselves to be part of the employees group

**[02:07:38.280] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001482)  
na nakikinabang din pag merong dinidistribute.

**[02:07:41.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001483)  
So, meron po nga yung ongoing technical working group between DOLE and NEA para maresolba po ito.

**[02:07:47.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001484)  
Meron lang kaunting usapin na ayaw ibigay ng NEA yung kanilang quote-unquote regulatory...

**[02:07:58.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001485)  
powers kung functions na madidilute kung sakaling pumasok sila sa ganitong kasunduan.

**[02:08:05.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001486)  
So, yung ganun pong aspeto ay ang aming pinag-uusapan ngayon.

**[02:08:11.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001487)  
It will take some time, but I think we're on the right path na mariresolba po ito.

**[02:08:17.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001488)  
May commitment po si NEA Administrator Almeda patungkol po dito.

**[02:08:22.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001489)  
Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary, gusto ko lang pong balaan kayo.

**[02:08:26.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001490)  
Ito pong si Director

**[02:08:28.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001491)  
Almeda, isang taon pa pong nangako sa amin dito sa Kongreso.

**[02:08:31.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001492)  
Pero hanggang ngayon po, hindi pa rin sinususpindi itong policy that allows the NEA to interfere

**[02:08:35.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001493)  
in the CBA of our workers' unions in this cooperative.

**[02:08:39.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001494)  
So, ang pakiusap po namin, while waiting for the tripartite policy,

**[02:08:42.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001495)  
baka pwede po magbigay ng issuean sa DOLE para sa ganun po.

**[02:08:45.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001496)  
Pigilan po ito pong hindi po legal na prosesong ito ng pagharang sa CBA ng ating mga frontliners

**[02:08:51.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001497)  
pwede ting sa electric co-op industry.

**[02:08:54.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001498)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. I'd like to proceed to...

**[02:08:58.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001499)  
September 29 po yung susunod na pagpupulong namin ng NEA.

**[02:09:02.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001500)  
September 29, sana po, even before that, baka pwede na po may issueans

**[02:09:06.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001501)  
to the effect na sinuspinde po natin at nirescind yung current policy.

**[02:09:11.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001502)  
Kasi po, kung mag-on-going pa po yung ating negotiations and dialogues,

**[02:09:17.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001503)  
it will take time bago po fully ma-rescind itong polisiyang ito.

**[02:09:21.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001504)  
Sana po, for the here and now, baka po pwedeng mahilingan ng isang issuance ang DOLE,

**[02:09:26.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001505)  
to allow our unions to proceed with their CBA without the interference of the NEA administration.

**[02:09:33.140] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001506)  
Pwede po akong makiusap, Your Honor.

**[02:09:35.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001507)  
Pwede po kaya sa September 29 ay magpadala kayo ng isang kinatawan

**[02:09:39.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001508)  
para makaharap din at maging bahagi ng usapin between DOLE and NEA?

**[02:09:45.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001509)  
Ang Power Centro po ay ipapadala natin sa natural dialogue na yan

**[02:09:51.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001510)  
para po talaga marinig po ninyo ng diretsyo yung concerns sa ating workers in the power industry.

**[02:09:55.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001511)  
Salamat po.

**[02:09:56.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001512)  
Let me go to our colleague from the TESDA, Secretary Benitez.

**[02:10:02.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001513)  
Sinabi po ng DTI noong nakaraang budget hearing po nila

**[02:10:05.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001514)  
that they are shifting their industrial policy from automotive towards electric vehicles.

**[02:10:11.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001515)  
Ang tanong po ng Philippine Metal Workers Alliance ay

**[02:10:14.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001516)  
may we ask if TESDA has ongoing plans to design TVET programs

**[02:10:18.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001517)

**[02:10:21.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001518)

**[02:10:26.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001519)  
standards and training regulations for EV, for electric vehicles po.

**[02:10:31.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001520)  
Ongoing po ang rollout ng training for EV in the energy sector.

**[02:10:36.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001521)  
I can submit to your officer the list of all the electric vehicle trainings

**[02:10:43.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001522)

**[02:10:51.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001523)

**[02:10:54.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001524)  
Meron pong training regulations ang TESDA.

**[02:10:56.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001525)  
Some of these were developed in partnerships po

**[02:10:59.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001526)

**[02:11:05.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001527)  
that is in advance in terms of having training modules.

**[02:11:08.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001528)  
So meron po to answer very succinctly.

**[02:11:12.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001529)  
At ibigay nyo na rin po sa amin kung saan pong areas

**[02:11:14.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001530)

**[02:11:17.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001531)  
para po makapasok sa industriyang ito.

**[02:11:19.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001532)  
Yes po.

**[02:11:20.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001533)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:11:22.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001534)  
Let me move on to my last point.

**[02:11:24.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001535)  
Sana po umabot 15 seconds.

**[02:11:26.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001536)  
May ipinabot pong concern ang mga kasama natin

**[02:11:29.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001537)  
mula sa National Union of Building and Construction Workers

**[02:11:32.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001538)  
at nagkaisa Labor Coalition.

**[02:11:34.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001539)  
Nabinbin daw po ang Construction Workers Training Program

**[02:11:37.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001540)  
nitong June 2026.

**[02:11:40.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001541)  
The interruption of the 2026 cycle could deprive up to 300 workers

**[02:11:45.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001542)  
of critical training opportunities.

**[02:11:48.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001543)  
Ang mabilis pong tanong, bakit hindi natuloy ang training na ito

**[02:11:51.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001544)

**[02:11:56.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001545)  
That would be my last question, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:02.750] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001546)  
Sorry, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:04.470] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001547)  
Sa amin pala yung tanong.

**[02:12:06.290] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001548)  
Sir, this is actually part of an agreement with the DPWH in Daga

**[02:12:12.090] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001549)

**[02:12:15.750] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001550)

**[02:12:19.470] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001551)

**[02:12:24.770] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001552)

**[02:12:26.630] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001553)  
We are waiting, sir, for the transfer of funds.

**[02:12:29.410] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001554)  
We are waiting for the transfer of funds from the DPWH for this purpose.

**[02:12:31.970] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001555)  
So we'll keep on following up, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:12:34.030] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001556)  
Thank you very much.

**[02:12:34.430] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-001557)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:35.690] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001558)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:12:38.130] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-001559)  
Thank you very much, Rep. Percy.

kasi,

**[02:19:04.680] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001690)  
The assailed order should not restrain respondents in implementing the wage order

**[02:19:09.160] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001691)

**[02:19:13.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001692)

**[02:19:20.200] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001693)

**[02:19:24.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001694)  
Rule 58 po.

**[02:19:26.160] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001695)  
Section 4B,

**[02:19:28.220] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001696)  
Rules of Court provides the issuance of writ is expressly relies on the fulfillment of certain conditions.

**[02:19:34.920] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001697)  
So, conditions in ikwanon.

**[02:19:36.460] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001698)  
There is a, if I may mistake, a suspensive condition.

**[02:19:40.220] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001699)  
Do not like to stand on, importante na magbayad siya ng bond.

**[02:19:44.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001700)  
So, there is certain condition which is the bond in this particular, in this moment.

**[02:19:48.140] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001701)  
This means that the order granting the P.I. or preliminary injunction,

**[02:19:54.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001702)  
does not, standing alone, operate as a writ itself.

**[02:19:58.800] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001703)  
The injunctive relief becomes legally operational only upon the fulfillment of the condition,

**[02:20:04.020] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001704)  
followed by the formal issuance of the corresponding writ.

**[02:20:07.340] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001705)  
Until these mandatory steps are fully satisfied,

**[02:20:10.560] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001706)  
the assailed order produces no binding injunctive effect.

**[02:20:14.320] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001707)

**[02:20:17.960] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001708)

**[02:29:57.000] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-001840)

**[02:30:23.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001847)

**[02:30:35.740] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001851)

**[02:30:39.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001852)

**[02:30:43.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001853)

**[02:30:46.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001854)  
60.

**[02:30:59.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001858)

**[02:31:21.300] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001862)

**[02:31:22.420] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-001863)

**[03:06:01.780] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002857)

**[03:06:05.780] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002859)

**[03:06:09.820] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002861)

**[03:06:17.800] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002865)

**[03:06:23.680] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002868)

**[03:06:25.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002869)

**[03:06:27.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002870)

**[03:06:41.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002877)

**[03:06:43.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002878)

**[03:06:45.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002879)

**[03:06:47.740] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002880)

**[03:06:49.600] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002881)

**[03:06:51.860] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002882)

**[03:06:53.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002883)

**[03:06:55.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002884)

**[03:06:57.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002885)  
agriculture. Ang tanong

**[03:06:59.900] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002886)  
po nyo eh, mayroon po

**[03:07:01.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002887)  
ba't kaming

**[03:07:02.700] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002888)  
wish list?

**[03:07:05.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002889)

**[03:07:07.740] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002890)  
Unang-una po eh, sana po

**[03:07:09.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002891)  
mabigyan po natin ng karapatang

**[03:07:11.460] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002892)  
budget in attention yung

**[03:07:13.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002893)  
ating mga youth employability

**[03:07:15.860] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002894)  
programs. Kasi po yung sila

**[03:07:17.680] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002895)  
yung nangangailangan ng

**[03:07:19.260] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002896)  
guidance in terms of

**[03:07:21.300] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002897)  
internship. Paano po sila

**[03:07:23.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002898)  
makagain ng eksperyensya

**[03:07:25.920] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002899)  
para matanggap sila?

**[03:07:27.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002900)  
Sa trabaho. So, karamihan

**[03:07:29.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002901)  
po kasi ng employers natin, pag wala

**[03:07:31.740] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002902)  
kang experience, hindi ka tatanggapin.

**[03:07:34.000] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002903)  
Pangalawa po, para may access

**[03:07:35.740] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002904)  
sila sa labor market information,

**[03:07:38.000] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002905)  
kasi marami po tayong mga bakante,

**[03:07:40.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002906)  
hindi po maka-absorb

**[03:07:41.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002907)  
ng ating mga kabataan dahil

**[03:07:43.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002908)  
hindi akma yung kanilang

**[03:07:45.880] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002909)  
educational

**[03:07:47.840] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002910)  
attainment, at

**[03:07:49.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002911)  
hindi po sila

**[03:07:51.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002912)  
well aware nung ating

**[03:07:54.040] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002913)  
vacancies

**[03:07:55.980] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002914)  
kasi marami din po tayong

**[03:07:57.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002915)  
hard to fill occupations,

**[03:08:00.100] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002916)  
meron din po tayong emerging occupations,

**[03:08:02.920] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002917)  
at meron din po tayong

**[03:08:04.080] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002918)  
ongoing na

**[03:08:06.160] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002919)  
vacancies na nagpa-pile up

**[03:08:08.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002920)  
po every year. So, kailangan po

**[03:08:10.460] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002921)  
natin paigtingin yung ating labor market

**[03:08:12.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002922)  
information, kaya po tayo

**[03:08:14.140] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002923)  
gusto natin paigtingin yung ating

**[03:08:16.360] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002924)  
public employment service offices, kasi

**[03:08:18.280] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002925)  
sila yung tumutulong nationwide.

**[03:08:20.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002926)  
Sila yung nag-offer ng

**[03:08:21.920] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002927)  
nagbibigay ng information, kung sino

**[03:08:24.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002928)  
yung mga job seekers, ano yung

**[03:08:26.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002929)  
profile nila,

**[03:08:27.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002930)  
ano yung mga vacancies, sino yung mga employers na naghahanap ng mga right skills nationwide.

**[03:08:34.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002931)  
Kaya po kailangan natin paigtingin ito.

**[03:08:36.620] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002932)  
So meron po kami yung aming sa Public Employment Service Office.

**[03:08:41.160] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002933)  
At kasama po dito, at in fact sa September 14 po nga,

**[03:08:45.080] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002934)  
meron kami nationwide job fairs, 84 job fairs nationwide.

**[03:08:50.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002935)  
At kami po ay magpaparticipate dito sa job fairs na to.

**[03:08:54.360] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002936)  
Pero hindi lang po sadyang job fair lang to.

**[03:08:58.320] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002937)  
Hindi yung pupunta lang yung mga tao para bigyan ng trabaho,

**[03:09:01.980] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002938)  
kundi bigyan sila ng guidance, career development support.

**[03:09:06.100] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002939)  
Ano ba yung tamang karir o ano pa yung tamang trabaho para sa'yo?

**[03:09:10.380] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002940)  
At kung hindi ka man matanggap na trabaho, bakit? Ano yung kakulangan?

**[03:09:14.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002941)  
So may advisory component po yan.

**[03:09:16.820] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002942)  
At kasama po namin yung test that's dun sa aming tinatawag na enhanced job fairs

**[03:09:22.140] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002943)  
kasi ika-guide sila.

**[03:09:23.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002944)  
So baka kailangan pa nila ng skills training o kailangan nila ng certification

**[03:09:27.840] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002945)  
dun para matanggap ng karapat-dapat na trabaho.

**[03:09:32.120] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002946)  
So yung aming labor market information system, ang peso,

**[03:09:37.720] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002947)  
ang aming youth employability program, sa to po yung programa namin dyan,

**[03:09:42.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002948)  
the famous government internship program,

**[03:09:45.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002949)  
which is very much in demand in the public service.

**[03:09:50.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002950)  
Nakita namin na tumaas for...

**[03:09:53.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002951)  
2027 and maraming salamat. Sana po mag-grant yung increase na yun.

**[03:09:58.160] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002952)  
Yung aming special program for the employment of students

**[03:10:01.140] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002953)  
kasi meron po kaming indigent students na nagtatrabaho during vacation

**[03:10:07.260] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002954)  
para lang makabayad ng tuition.

**[03:10:10.700] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002955)  
And yung aming job start, ito po yung kailangan po namin i-link sila closely with employers.

**[03:10:16.320] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002956)  
Dyan po pumapasok yung mga critical skills at internship with employers.

**[03:10:21.380] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002957)  
Pero hindi lang po technical training.

**[03:10:23.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002958)  
We also guide them with soft skills.

**[03:10:26.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002959)  
Yung teamwork.

**[03:10:28.720] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002960)  
Ngayon po kailangan ng AI.

**[03:10:30.900] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002961)  
Lahat po tayo kailangan ng AI training, AI orientation.

**[03:10:35.320] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002962)  
So yun po kasama po yan dun sa aming mga programa.

**[03:10:40.320] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002963)  
Tapos of course we have to adjust our programs to climate change, renewable energy,

**[03:10:47.020] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002964)  
kasi siya po yung mga in-demand na mga skills ngayon.

**[03:10:50.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002965)  
And then of course yung nabanggit na po ni...

**[03:10:53.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002966)  
ni Secretary Tolentino, yung aming pag-support sa micro, small, and medium enterprises

**[03:10:58.960] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002967)  
through our adjustment measures program po.

**[03:11:02.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-002968)  
Yan po.

**[03:11:02.920] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002969)  
Thank you po, Yusek.

**[03:11:04.120] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002970)

**[03:11:08.340] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002971)

**[03:11:11.660] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002972)

**[03:11:18.940] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002973)

**[03:11:22.800] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002974)

**[03:11:23.500] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002975)

**[03:11:27.700] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002976)

**[03:11:30.640] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002977)

**[03:11:33.380] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002978)

**[03:11:38.560] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002979)

**[03:11:44.120] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002980)

**[03:11:46.980] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002981)

**[03:11:49.540] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002982)

**[03:11:51.680] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002983)  
agad pong tumutulong...

**[03:11:53.500] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002984)

**[03:11:54.620] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002985)  
Maraming salamat po sa mga programa ninyong ganyan.

**[03:11:58.340] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002986)  
Dahil hindi po ako nung...

**[03:12:00.200] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002987)  
katulad nung mga nakaraan,

**[03:12:02.180] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002988)  
bago pa po kayo dumating,

**[03:12:04.080] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002989)  
nakita po natin na lagi na lang inooffer po.

**[03:12:07.140] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002990)  
Keri...

**[03:12:07.700] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002991)  
which is sa totoo lang,

**[03:12:09.400] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002992)  
yung sa ginagawa ninyo po ngayon,

**[03:12:12.600] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002993)  
yung sa pagja-job match,

**[03:12:14.360] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002994)

**[03:12:16.940] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002995)

**[03:12:18.620] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002996)  
Sana po ipagpatuloy ninyo po yung inyong ginagawa.

**[03:12:21.160] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002997)  
At ganun din po,

**[03:12:22.280] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002998)  
nagpapasalamat tayo kay...

**[03:12:23.500] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-002999)  
Francis,

**[03:12:24.300] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003000)  
alam po natin na napakahirap po

**[03:12:25.980] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003001)  
nang napasukan ninyong departamento.

**[03:12:28.460] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003002)  
Alam po namin na...

**[03:12:30.400] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003003)  
sabi nga nila,

**[03:12:31.220] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003004)  
is puro sakit ng ulo,

**[03:12:32.620] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003005)  
pero naniniwala ako,

**[03:12:34.040] Rep. Ramon Jolo III B. Revilla** (asr-003006)  
ganyan talaga kapiten nyo.

**[03:16:26.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003087)

**[03:16:36.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003088)

**[03:16:56.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003089)

**[03:17:22.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003091)

good afternoon okay i only have two points to raise mr chair one is the establishment of the

**[03:30:15.580] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003480)  
prc regional office for the negros island region and the other one is the impact of ai on the bpo

**[03:30:23.640] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003481)  
and call center workers it has been two years since the creation of the nir which was intended

**[03:30:31.820] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003482)  
to bring government services closer to our people streamline operations and ensure more efficient

**[03:30:39.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003483)  
service delivery yet prc remains divided between two regions the prc dumaguete office continues to

**[03:30:49.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003484)  
report to region 7 in cebu while prc bacolod

**[03:30:53.520] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003485)  
reports to region 6 in iloilo may we ask the commission for the status of the establishment

**[03:31:00.040] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003486)  
of a prc nir regional office and why after two years has this still not been realized

**[03:31:08.800] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003487)  
this is particularly important given the volume of prc services needed by our constituents

**[03:31:16.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003488)  
actually last august 29 to 30 dumaguete city served as one of the testing

**[03:31:24.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003491)  
for the philippine nurses licensure examination with around 4 000 examinees

**[03:31:29.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003492)  
having a dedicated nir office would greatly improve coordination logistics and accessibility

**[03:31:36.560] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003493)  
for our professionals and examinees can the commission provide a clear timeline for the

**[03:31:42.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003494)  
establishment of the prc nir regional office thank you mr chair

**[03:32:11.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003498)

**[03:32:18.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003499)  
through the years so yeah your honor

can we hear from uh chairperson charito zamora

**[03:32:27.690] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003500-split-2)  
thank you mr chair with your permission and with

**[03:32:30.080] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003501)  
the permission of our uh good secretary um your honor for uh the nir we actually have already in

our nep uh 3.617 million allotment for tier one so that's for 2027 mom and then um we uh of course

**[03:32:51.060] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003503)  
uh also uh requested uh in our tier two uh via appeal po mom nag appeal po kami sa dbm

for a higher uh budget which is 12 million 480 000 po para po dito sa uh transition ng ating

**[03:33:10.460] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003505)  
region yun po mga status ngayon maraming salamat po

**[03:33:17.440] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003505-split-2)  
thank you thank you um what when would that be

**[03:33:21.060] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003506)  
mam uh for 2027 uh we'll be able uh since we have uh already given in our nep a budget of 3

**[03:33:31.120] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003507)  
million 617 000 so mas malaking office po ang mga ma-occupy in dumaguete because right now

talagang maliit po eh oo so yun po ang gagawin natin for 2027 we'll have bigger office po para

so so magmamana yung um regional office talaga kay as of now it's still a parang satellite lang

so uh bigger po so somehow for transition sa talagang full-fledged regional office po so

**[03:34:06.780] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003512)  
doon naman po papunta yung ating track mom so by 2027 we'll have bigger definitely uh space

**[03:34:13.900] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003513)  
to accommodate po more professionals po, mga kliyente po.

**[03:34:19.710] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003513-split-2)

**[03:34:23.050] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003514)

**[03:34:27.480] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-003515)  
at 3.6 million po yung budget lang na inaibigay po sa atin at least sa nep po but if uh kung

po namin na kung pwedeng meron ng magman talaga

Okay, thank you for that.

**[03:34:48.700] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003517-split-4)  
And okay, the second

**[03:34:51.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003518)  
topic is mr chair on the impact of the artificial intelligence on our bpo and call center workers

**[03:34:59.200] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003519)  
there are now more than 15 000 workers employed in the ict bpo industry in negros oriental

**[03:35:06.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003520)  
according to the local ict bpo industry and the sector continues to expand the bpo industry has

**[03:35:16.660] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003521)  
been one of the major sources of employment not only for negros oriental

**[03:35:21.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003522)  
but for filipinos no particularly for young workers and those entering the workforce but

**[03:35:27.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003523)  
with the rapid adoption of ai many routine customer service and support functions are

**[03:35:33.520] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003524)  
increasingly being automated i understand that dolly is already pursuing upskilling

**[03:35:40.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003525)  
reskilling and adjust transition framework for workers affected by technological change

**[03:35:46.940] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003526)  
my question is does dolly have a specific program

**[03:35:51.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003527)  
strategy for bpo and call center workers who may be displaced or whose jobs may be significantly

**[03:35:58.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003528)  
altered by ai i would also like to ask whether dolly is working with the bpo industry to require

**[03:36:06.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003529)  
or encourage companies adopting ai to provide meaningful reskilling and redeployment opportunities

**[03:36:14.520] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003530)  
for their existing employees because our objective would not simply be to prepare for unemployment

**[03:36:21.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003531)  
we should prepare them to work alongside AI

**[03:36:24.720] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003532)  
and to move into the higher-valued jobs that AI itself is creating.

**[03:36:30.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-003533)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:36:34.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003534)  
Go ahead.

**[03:36:34.740] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003535)  
We'll require Usec Torres and then Sekiko to respond.

**[03:36:39.600] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003536)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[03:36:43.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003537)  
Ma'am, pwedeng palapit yung...

**[03:36:45.040] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003538)  
Some further information on AI.

**[03:36:47.780] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003539)  
In the Philippine labor market,

**[03:36:50.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003540)  
we're saying that AI is transforming jobs

**[03:36:53.720] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003541)  
rather than outright replacing them.

**[03:36:58.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003542)  
And with our study, we've seen very small unemployment rates,

**[03:37:02.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003543)  
3 to 4 percent,

**[03:37:04.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003544)  
and we've identified which occupations these are.

**[03:37:10.000] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003545)  
More of the affected jobs and roles,

**[03:37:11.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003546)  
nabanggit niyo na po yung BPO, IT-BPM,

**[03:37:15.860] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003547)  
clerical and administrative support,

**[03:37:19.340] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003548)  
finance accounts,

**[03:37:20.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003549)  
accounting and related processing,

**[03:37:23.120] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003550)  
other areas,

**[03:37:25.380] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003551)  
certain sales and service roles,

**[03:37:28.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003552)  
and ang geographic concentration,

**[03:37:30.880] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003553)  
NCR, Central Luzon, Calabar Zone,

**[03:37:33.140] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003554)  
nang banggit niyo po sa Visayas.

**[03:37:36.060] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003555)  
What are the jobs being created?

**[03:37:38.960] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003556)  
AI-related specialists.

**[03:37:40.420] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003557)  
Kaya po yung ating upskilling, reskilling,

**[03:37:43.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003558)  
are very important dahil we have to upgrade

**[03:37:46.700] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003559)  
the occupations of these jobs,

**[03:37:50.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003560)  
which are affected by AI.

**[03:37:52.280] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003561)  
So may mga bagong occupations,

**[03:37:54.280] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003562)  
and we have to be really alert

**[03:37:56.380] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003563)  
kung ano po yung occupations na to.

**[03:37:58.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003564)  
AI-related specialists,

**[03:38:00.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003565)  
masyado pong specialized sila,

**[03:38:02.840] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003566)  
from engineers, AI trainers, auditors,

**[03:38:05.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003567)  
we have po the list we can provide you the information.

**[03:38:09.120] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003568)  
Data analysts,

**[03:38:10.460] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003569)  
high-value BPO services,

**[03:38:13.700] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003570)  
and industry groups,

**[03:38:14.880] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003571)  
and we have really been working closely with IBPAP

**[03:38:17.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003572)  
ever since two years ago,

**[03:38:20.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003573)  
we've been already working with them,

**[03:38:22.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003574)  
providing them also support,

**[03:38:24.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003575)  
not only for training,

**[03:38:25.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003576)  
but also supporting youth

**[03:38:28.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003577)  
who are going through this different occupation.

**[03:38:32.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003578)  
So what are our other programs sa aming isa DOLE?

**[03:38:36.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003579)  
Of course, employment facilitation, importante.

**[03:38:39.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003580)  
How do we guide these adults who are displaced?

**[03:38:42.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003581)  
Saan ang occupation sila na-displaced?

**[03:38:45.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003582)  
Ano yung saan sila pwedeng pumuntang

**[03:38:47.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003583)  
occupations na AI-related?

**[03:38:50.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003584)  
Kaya importante po yung ating

**[03:38:52.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003585)  
Public Employment Service Offices.

**[03:38:54.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003586)  
Skills development,

**[03:38:56.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003587)  
Tekniko will give details on this,

**[03:38:59.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003588)  
but also social protection,

**[03:39:01.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003589)  
kasi po yung mga AI-adjusted unemployment insurance,

**[03:39:05.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003590)  
kailangan namin yung iredi.

**[03:39:08.200] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-003591)  
And then ensure also gender-responsive measures,

**[04:45:00.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003919)  
Thank you, Representative Antonio Tino. We now move back to the majority, which is I'd like to call on Representative Tony Roman.

**[04:45:12.660] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003920)  
Good afternoon, Secretary, and to Dole and Testa. When your NEP for 2027 fell compared to the 2026 GAA by about 8%, I would assume that prior to the issuance or release of the proposed NEP for 2027,

**[04:45:40.000] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003921)  
you have submitted programs.

**[04:45:42.660] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003922)  
You have submitted programs that have been removed because not all 100% are being given. What are the programs that you have submitted that because your NEP for 2027 fell by 8%, it was removed and you want to return it?

**[04:46:04.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003923)  
I would defer to Undersecretary Warren Platt. It's in his details.

**[04:46:12.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003924)  
You're referring to programs not part of ILS, not part of NWPC. NLRC went down, PRC went up. Kayo na po mag-provide ng dito.

**[04:46:24.220] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003925)  
Thank you, Secretary. With the permission of the Secretary, yung mga nakat po sa budget ng Dole ay ang mga sumusunod po.

**[04:46:39.750] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003926)  
Sa case po ng Dole proper, yung pong Tupad program.

**[04:46:46.470] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003927)  
Sa livelihood program. At saka po yung computerization program po namin kasi yung capital outlay po ay nag-scale back na po, MOE na lang po.

**[04:46:56.830] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003928)  
And then sa case ng ILS naman po increase, ang NCMB naman po ay decrease dahil sa non-approval ng proposed additional ICT requirements

**[04:47:06.350] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003929)  
which is under the maintenance and other operating expenses and capital outlay at hindi rin po kami nabigyan sa NCMB ng proposed funding for the acquisition of ICT.

**[04:47:16.470] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003930)  
For the NLRC po, yung net decrease naman ay due to the following retirement of five commissioners with cotermius employees, non-approval ng proposed additional ICT requirements,

**[04:47:31.410] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003931)  
non-approval din ng proposed replacement ng fully depreciated motor vehicles.

**[04:47:37.110] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003932)  
Sa case ng NWPC, it's an increase. PRC is an increase.

**[04:47:42.150] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003933)  
TESDA is a decrease. In terms of MOE, it's a substantial reduction in the provisions of the implementation of the program such as the special training for employment program or STEP,

**[04:47:55.810] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003934)  
training for work scholarship program or TWSP, and lifelong employability and advancement program, micro-credentials for near-hires, in-service workers in the future workforce.

**[04:48:07.430] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003935)  
And also a reduction in their foreign-assisted project.

**[04:48:12.150] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003936)  
Particularly the supporting innovation in the Philippine Technical and Vocational Education and Training System or SIPTIVETS.

**[04:48:19.610] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003937)  
And capital outlay, no provision for their ICT and vehicle as well.

**[04:48:25.190] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003938)  
Thanks po.

**[04:48:26.510] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003939)  
So, in particular, yung computerization, paano ito nakakapekto sa trabaho ng DOLE?

**[04:48:34.410] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003940)  
Yung sa computerization naman po, sa 2026 may budget naman po kami.

**[04:48:41.010] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003942)  
Ang hindi na lang po na-provide for next year, yung mga capital outlay na pambili ng mga hardware like computers, kasi meron na po provided for 2026.

**[04:48:50.730] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003943)  
At may mga natira pa po na 2025 na continuing po na this year na dapat na rin po naming maubos, pambili rin po ng mga hardware components such as desktop, laptop, computers.

**[04:49:01.570] DOLE Undersecretary Warren M. Miclat** (asr-003944)  
At ito po ay mga nakapila na po for the procurement process po.

**[04:49:05.770] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003945)  
Mr. Secretary, kanina yung tinanong ni Kong Jolo.

**[04:49:09.130] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003946)  
Yung programa na naisip ninyo, yung NALJ or National Academy of Labor Justice at saka yung Labor Attorney's Office.

**[04:49:19.390] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003947)  
Ito ho ba kasama na mga programang isinumiti nyo pero hindi na pondohan?

**[04:49:28.590] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003948)  
Kasama po yan.

**[04:49:29.750] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003949)  
In fact, we wrote a separate letter to the Speaker of the House, the members of...

**[04:49:39.130] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003950)  
And committees and pretty soon with the Senate President himself.

**[04:49:43.310] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003951)  
So, naniniwala po kami na kailangan ito. Ano lang naman po?

**[04:49:48.630] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003952)  
50 million plus 75 million.

**[04:49:52.190] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003953)  
125 million po to address the...

**[04:49:56.490] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003954)  
One, to address the needs of the workers in Labor Attorney's Office para may free legal assistance sila nationwide to hire 64 lawyers.

**[04:50:06.790] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003955)  
And then yung pangalawa...

**[04:50:09.130] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003956)  
Para magkaroon tayo ng isang academy na magte-training sa mga mediators, conciliators, labor arbiters, etc., etc.

**[04:50:19.070] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-003957)  
And continuing education na rin po.

**[04:50:20.610] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003958)

**[05:00:24.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004078)

**[05:00:30.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004079)

**[05:04:32.910] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004125)

**[05:04:41.550] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004126)

**[05:04:56.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004128)

**[05:05:00.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004129)

**[05:05:08.930] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004130)

**[05:05:11.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004131)

**[05:05:18.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004133)

**[05:05:21.810] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004134)

**[05:05:33.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004136)

**[05:05:38.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004137)

**[05:05:43.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-004138)

Mr. Chair, I would need to know which schools that you referred to is the first question because there is a process by which allocations for scholarships are put against schools, their absorptive capacity, and the qualifications that they offer specific to an area.

**[05:18:15.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004407)  
That's the first, that's the first point sir.

**[05:18:17.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004408)

Okay, thank you very much, Sec.

**[05:19:58.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004426)

**[05:20:01.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004427)

**[05:20:14.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004429)

**[05:20:21.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004430)

**[05:20:40.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004432)

**[05:20:42.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004433)

**[05:20:54.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004434)  
In that particular program, we're actually looking into digitized program registration.

**[05:21:00.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004435)  
So information about qualifications or programs, the enterprises where they can apply are actually in that particular online system.

**[05:21:10.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004436)  
And two more.

**[05:21:11.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004437)  
Integrators is actually one program that TESDA is looking at as far as the EBIT is concerned

**[05:21:19.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004438)  
because we wanted also to onboard micro, small and medium enterprises

**[05:21:25.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004439)  
because we know that micro, small and medium enterprises do not have,

**[05:21:28.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004440)

**[05:21:35.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004441)

**[05:21:40.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Nelly Nita N. Dillera** (asr-004442)  
who can assist the MSMEs.

**[05:23:06.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004460)  
Thank you so much, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:09.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004461)  
And first of all, I'd like to congratulate our new Secretary of State,

**[05:23:13.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004462)  
our new Secretary of Labor.

**[05:23:15.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004463)  
One of the best senators and pinak-mabayit din po.

**[05:23:18.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004464)  
And we are very happy to have him now at the DOLE.

**[05:23:22.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004465)  
So my first question is, we are very worried about children and the internet.

**[05:23:29.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004466)  
And especially its relationship po to violence and other issues regarding children's mental health.

**[05:23:38.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004467)  
Can you tell us more about Labor Advisory No. 12?

**[05:23:41.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004468)  
And child influencers.

**[05:23:43.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004469)  
Worse, hindi lang sila nagkoconsume sa internet.

**[05:23:46.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004470)  
E sila pa po yung nagpe-perform sa internet.

**[05:23:49.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004471)  
I'm just only hoping that it is safe.

**[05:23:52.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004472)  
Yung ginagawa po nila ay hindi po exploitation ng children.

**[05:23:57.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004473)  
In fact, by their own parents.

**[05:23:59.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004474)  
Kasi kumikita po ito.

**[05:24:01.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004475)  
Nagulat po ako na yung kaklase daw ng anak ko, so ni grade 9.

**[05:24:04.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004476)  
A makeup influencer na raw.

**[05:24:06.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004477)  
At marami na raw kinikita.

**[05:24:08.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004478)  
So can you let us know what is the DOLE's stand on this?

**[05:24:11.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004479)  
And what are you doing po to protect our children

**[05:24:14.370] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004480)  
from not only consuming but performing internet activities?

**[05:24:19.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004481)  
Ang salamat po, Congresswoman.

**[05:24:22.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004482)  
I-issue lang po namin hindi katagalan yung Advisory No. 12.

**[05:24:28.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004483)  
At ang nilalaman po nito ay para bigyan din at laman yung protection para sa ating mga kabataan below 15.

**[05:24:35.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004484)  
Dalawa po ang aspeto nito.

**[05:24:37.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004485)  
Yung nagtatrabaho po na kontrolado ng magulang.

**[05:24:39.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004486)  
At yung nagtatrabaho po

**[05:24:40.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004487)  
At yung nagtatrabaho po kontrolado ng employer.

**[05:24:43.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004488)  
Sa dalawang branch po ay amin po sinisigurado na ang kabataang Pilipino ay hindi ma-exploit.

**[05:24:51.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004489)  
Hindi magtatrabaho ng between 10 to 6 ng madaling araw.

**[05:24:56.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004490)  
At mangangailangan po ng permit kung ito ay nagtatrabaho sa isang employer.

**[05:25:02.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004491)  
At mangangailangan din po na siguraduhin yung kikitain niya 20% lang ang mapupunta po sa pamilya.

**[05:25:09.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004492)  
At yung 80% ay mapupunta sa kanyang pag-aaral, sa kanyang well-being, sa kanyang kalusugan.

**[05:25:15.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004493)  
Nang sa ganun po hindi po ma-exploit yung kabataang Pilipino.

**[05:25:19.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004494)  
Lalawang lalo na yung pumapasok sa dalawang uri po yan.

**[05:25:22.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004495)  
Entertainment yung isa.

**[05:25:24.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004496)  
At yung isa po ay talagang nagsusuporta doon sa mga advertisements, etc., etc.

**[05:25:29.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004497)  
Lumalabas sa mga television shows.

**[05:25:31.370] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004498)  
At makikita natin pinagahanap buhay na ng maaga.

**[05:29:02.280] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-004551)

**[05:30:45.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004577)

**[05:30:50.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004578)

**[05:30:53.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004579)  
So, this is a form of short-term employment.

**[05:30:55.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004580)

**[05:31:02.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004581)

**[05:31:04.660] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004582)

**[05:31:06.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004583)

**[05:31:10.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004584)

**[05:31:15.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004585)

**[05:31:24.720] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004587)

**[05:31:26.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004588)

**[05:31:29.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004589)  
And then, kailangan po malinaw yung deliverables.

**[05:31:31.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004590)

**[05:31:34.480] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004591)

**[05:31:38.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004592)

**[05:31:45.100] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004593)

**[05:31:49.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004594)

**[05:31:52.340] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004595)

**[05:31:54.720] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004596)

**[05:31:59.160] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004597)  
Naisip po na isang principal namin, sila po yung magbubukas ng mga bag na mga bata.

**[05:32:04.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004598)  
So, that was supposed to be a joke.

**[05:32:08.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004599)  
So, ang nagmamonitor po sa kanila, yung DepEd.

**[05:32:12.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004600)

**[05:32:15.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004601)  
So, you need to have a monitoring.

**[05:32:18.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004602)  
So, kailangan po talaga yan.

**[05:32:19.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004603)  
And then, I would like to share some.

**[05:32:39.340] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004611)

**[05:32:40.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004612)

**[05:32:42.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004613)  
And moving forward, yan po yung tinanim nila.

**[05:32:45.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004614)

**[05:32:48.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004615)

**[05:32:51.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004616)

**[05:32:56.840] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004618)

**[05:33:03.100] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004620)

**[05:33:11.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004623)

**[05:33:20.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004625)

**[05:33:23.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004626)

**[05:33:29.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004627)

**[05:33:31.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004628)  
So, there has been monitoring.

**[05:33:33.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004629)

**[05:33:37.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004630)  
And lastly, maraming salamat.

**[05:33:40.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004631)

**[05:33:45.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004632)

**[05:33:50.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004633)

**[05:33:53.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004634)

**[05:33:58.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004635)

**[05:34:02.160] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004636)
