**[05:35:17.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004658)  
Hindi, kinuha ko talaga lahat na nanganak.

**[05:35:20.480] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004659)  
So, ngayon, marami naman hong nagpapabunti.

**[05:35:22.600] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004660)  
So, unfortunately, nangyayari din po yun.

**[05:35:26.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004661)  
So, thank you so much, Sec, and sa Dole family.

**[05:35:31.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004662)  
Thank you so much.

**[05:35:32.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004663)  
And sana po, mas marami pa po tayong matulungan.

**[05:35:35.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004664)  
And tama po kayo, we need to be flexible to the times din po.

**[05:35:38.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004665)  
Kasi ang dami pong nangyayaring disasters.

**[05:35:41.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004666)  
So, maraming salamat po.

**[05:35:42.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004667)  
And more power po sa Dole.

**[05:35:45.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004668)  
And babalik po ako for the test.

**[05:35:47.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004669)  
Thank you so much.

**[05:35:49.440] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004670)  
Thank you, Rep. Anna York.

**[05:35:52.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004671)  
Ngayon, may president na rin tayo.

**[05:35:54.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004672)  
Hindi ko pa nagagawa yun yung ano.

**[05:35:56.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004673)  
Kaya lang, mabuntis muna.

**[05:35:59.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004674)  
Before I continue,

**[05:36:01.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004675)  
Before I continue, I'd like to, the chair wishes to acknowledge Rep. Renee Louise Co.

**[05:36:06.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004676)  
And we move along to the minority.

**[05:36:09.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004677)  
I may call on to Rep. Iris Montes.

**[05:36:13.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004678)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:36:14.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004679)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[05:36:16.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004680)  
The concern raised by Congresswoman Anna York Bondok is actually

**[05:36:22.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004681)  
one of my concerns also.

**[05:36:24.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004682)  
In fact, last budget hearing, I already raised that to the Dole.

**[05:36:27.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004683)  
And I am glad, ibida ko na lang po sa Region 4A,

**[05:36:32.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004684)  
meron ng mga pagbabagong ginawa sa TUPAD.

**[05:36:36.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004685)  
And like Congresswoman Anna York, meron din po sa amin yung gulayan.

**[05:36:41.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004686)  
We do that and we are piloting the 31 days po na TUPAD program.

**[05:36:47.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004687)  
Hindi lang po 7 days, 10 days, kundi 31 days.

**[05:36:51.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004688)  
Para to ensure,

**[05:36:52.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004689)  
na yung inumpisahan, makikita yung resulta at the end.

**[05:36:55.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004690)  
And they committed na tatapusin, kahit wala na po silang sweldo,

**[05:37:00.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004691)  
yung aming mga kababaihan, na ipagpapatuloy po

**[05:37:03.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004692)  
yung community gardening and planting po nila.

**[05:37:07.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004693)  
We also did coastal cleanup.

**[05:37:10.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004694)  
And ngayon po sa Region 4A, meron po silang geo-tagging na po ng TUPAD.

**[05:37:16.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004695)  
So nakikita in real time kung mga nagtatrabaho po ba yung mga,

**[05:37:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004696)  
yung sa TUPAD.

**[05:37:24.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004697)  
Pero may mga challenges kasi there are areas na wala pong signal.

**[05:37:28.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004698)  
So walang geo-tagging.

**[05:37:30.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004699)  
But I commend the DOLE for listening na magkaroon ng pagbabago at reform

**[05:37:37.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004700)  
sa pag-implement ng TUPAD.

**[05:37:40.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004701)  
Moving on po, yung question ko po talagang ito.

**[05:37:46.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004702)  
It's often said that our human resource, our people,

**[05:37:51.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004703)  
is our most important human resource.

**[05:37:54.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004704)  
But today, we are confronted po with a sad news.

**[05:37:59.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004705)  
A few hours ago, it's all over the news,

**[05:38:02.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004706)  
that our unemployment for July has risen to 6% from 4.9,

**[05:38:08.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004707)  
translating to about 314 Filipinos who are out of jobs.

**[05:38:13.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004708)  
3 million Filipinos.

**[05:38:18.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004709)  
Our labor force participation rate has also,

**[05:38:21.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004710)  
has also dipped to 63.6 from 65.

**[05:38:26.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004711)  
And then we have a rising unemployment rate.

**[05:38:30.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004712)  
So we are in a very trying times and challenging times.

**[05:38:36.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004713)  
So I understand.

**[05:38:37.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004714)  
Even the DEPDEP admitted that we have to scale up our efforts

**[05:38:47.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004715)  
to create more jobs and quality jobs at that.

**[05:38:51.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004716)  
But the DOLE has also said that it will intensify its efforts.

**[05:38:56.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004717)  
Given po, Mr. Secretary, ng budget na meron tayo,

**[05:39:01.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004718)  
paano po natin actually may intensify yung efforts natin?

**[05:39:06.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004719)  
Especially po, sa figure na ito po yata,

**[05:39:09.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004720)  
yung mga youth dyan, yung mga bagong graduates,

**[05:39:12.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004721)  
hindi daw nagkaroon ng pagkakataon na makasali

**[05:39:15.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004722)  
kasi sa labor force, kasi walang available na trabaho.

**[05:39:19.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004723)  
So how do we plan?

**[05:39:20.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004724)  
Given our budget,

**[05:39:21.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004725)  
how do we scale up employment, especially for the youth?

**[05:39:25.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004726)  
Tama po kayo, Your Honor.

**[05:39:26.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004727)  
Medyo hindi maganda yung lumabas ngayon na labor force survey.

**[05:39:35.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004728)  
Tumaas.

**[05:39:36.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004729)  
Nag-decline yung employment rate from 94% to 94.7% in July.

**[05:39:42.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004730)  
Unemployment rate increased to 6% from 5.3.

**[05:39:46.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004731)  
So yung unemployed persons rose from 551,000 year-on-year

**[05:39:51.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004732)  
from 2.59 million to 3.14 million.

**[05:39:56.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004733)  
Ito po, mga katabi ko, Your Honor,

**[05:39:58.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004734)  
mamaya siguro para makasagot na si Tesla Secretary,

**[05:40:03.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004735)  
mapapaliwanag niya yung ginagawa po doon sa Tesla Skills Passport

**[05:40:08.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004736)  
na inulunsad ngayong taon na to.

**[05:40:10.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004737)  
Ganon din po yung aming nilagdaan, nakaalaman tungo sa kabuhayan.

**[05:40:15.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004738)  
Ang tinutukoy ko po rito,

**[05:40:17.560] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004739)  
sinasabi nyo kasi, bumaba yung amin.

**[05:40:20.120] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004740)  
Bumaba rin yung kanila.

**[05:40:21.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004741)  
Pero pag kami nagsanib po,

**[05:40:23.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004742)  
merong kaukulang risulta siguro na pwedeng asahan yung Dole-Tesda convergence.

**[05:40:30.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004743)  
So papaliwanag po yan ni Secretary Kiko mamaya.

**[05:40:34.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004744)  
But let me ask Usec Torres to give a more detailed quantified analysis

**[05:40:44.300] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004745)  
of what really came out of that LFS, that survey today.

**[05:40:51.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004746)  
Maraming salamat po, Secretary, Your Honor.

**[05:40:55.360] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004747)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[05:40:57.940] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004748)  
Napaka-importante pong question kasi nga,

**[05:41:00.780] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004749)  
we're concerned na tumaas yung ating unemployment rate.

**[05:41:04.340] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004750)  
At marami pong causes at unang-una nga nabanggit po nyo yung ating kabataan.

**[05:41:09.400] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004751)  
Ang nangyari po ay na-increase yung new entrance sa labor market.

**[05:41:14.260] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004752)  
And if you recall, yung ating gross domestic product,

**[05:41:17.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004753)  
nag-grow lang ang economy sa 2.3%.

**[05:41:21.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004754)  
So dahil mabagal yung growth, mabagal din yung industry,

**[05:41:25.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004755)  
hindi po nakakatch up yung ating employment.

**[05:41:29.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004756)  
So bumagal din.

**[05:41:31.240] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004757)  
Kaya dumami po yung unemployed na youth.

**[05:41:36.120] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004758)  
Kaya po napaka-importante na tingnan po natin to,

**[05:41:39.760] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004759)  
kung sino yung mga youth, saan sila located,

**[05:41:43.140] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004760)  
at kung saan malaki yung job losses.

**[05:41:45.300] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004761)  
Na-identify po namin yung mga job losses kasama dyan po ang agriculture,

**[05:41:50.860] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004762)  
manufacturing.

**[05:41:51.520] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004763)  
Kung saan natin dapat i-enhance yung employment.

**[05:41:56.540] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004764)  
Saka yung ating mga youth para sa ating, ano,

**[05:41:59.880] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004765)  
to find out kung saan sila pwede ma-employ.

**[05:42:02.900] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004766)  
I-link po yung ating employment, school to employment transition,

**[05:42:07.060] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004767)  
yung po yung in-emphasize nga po ni Secretary Tolentino,

**[05:42:10.660] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004768)  
na-enhance po natin yung transition from school to work ng ating mga kabataan.

**[05:42:15.500] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004769)  
Dumami po kasi yung ating graduates, kaya talagang kailangan natin i-catch up yan.

**[05:42:20.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004770)  
At ang isa pong ating kailangang i-intensify,

**[05:42:24.980] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004771)  
yung ating youth employability program.

**[05:42:27.260] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004772)  
Nabanggit ko na po earlier, napaka-useful yung ating government internship program,

**[05:42:34.880] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004773)  
na kung saan binibigyan natin pagkakataon yung ating mga youth na ma-employ sa ating government service

**[05:42:42.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004774)  
and to have careers in government service,

**[05:42:45.040] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004775)  
yung ating special program for the employment of students,

**[05:42:49.180] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004776)  
at saka po yung ating jobs.

**[05:42:50.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004777)  
At yung ating job start na napaka-famous po sa ating mga mga kabataan.

**[05:42:55.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004778)  
Yung isa po ay yung ating i-intensify yung ating support through our employment facilitation

**[05:43:01.960] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004779)  
kasi kailangan silang mag-guide kung saan talaga yung employment and vacancies.

**[05:43:07.220] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004780)  
Maraming po tayong occupations na hard to fill and emerging at kailangan natin i-guide sila.

**[05:43:13.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004781)  
Dun po sa ating two frameworks, existing frameworks ay Trabajo Para sa Bayan Plan,

**[05:43:20.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004782)  
and labor and employment plan.

**[05:43:22.060] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004783)  
Naka-identify po tayo ng mga key growth centers nationwide at kung saan malaki yung employment

**[05:43:30.580] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004784)  
at dun po natin kailangan i-guide from education, our one education which is DepEd, TESDA, and CHED

**[05:43:38.440] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004785)  
to work together to guide them for employment.

**[05:43:41.840] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004786)  
So yun po yung ating ginagawa sa DOLE.

**[05:43:44.640] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004787)  
Si Sec. Tolentino po will...

**[05:43:47.480] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004788)  
Ay, ay, ay, Sec. Benitez will...

**[05:43:50.560] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004789)  
Give you details on kailangan po rin natin yung upskilling and reskilling and also our internship apprenticeship program.

**[05:43:58.460] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004790)  
Sige po.

**[05:43:59.920] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004791)  
Siguro unahin ko na lang that I will echo you Sec. Torres' position or statement

**[05:44:06.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004792)  
that the slowdown in the macroeconomic of the country has also made itself felt in the employment figures.

**[05:44:13.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004793)  
And so what it really will require is to intensify two things.

**[05:44:18.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004794)  
Actually three things na na-mention na rin.

**[05:44:20.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004795)  
The first is obviously matching the job openings to the people that might be looking for a job.

**[05:44:27.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004796)  
Sa area-based demand-driven po namin, malinaw po na meron pong more job openings, no?

**[05:44:38.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004797)  
In fact, then we have unemployed Filipinos, which really suggests that the job openings that DOLE itself actually puts together

**[05:44:47.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004798)  
in the peso, in the public employment service offices,

**[05:44:50.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004799)  
that information doesn't reach the right people who might be the right fit for those jobs.

**[05:44:55.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004800)  
So anything that we can do to improve that and intensify

**[05:45:00.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004801)  
that link, the faster we can address unemployment, at least in terms of employment per se.

**[05:45:07.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004802)  
The second is, of course, nabanggit rin ni Yusek Torres, is the job skills mismatching with regards to DepEd, TESDA, and CHED

**[05:45:17.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004803)  
to make sure that our youth actually have the right skills for the kind of jobs that are available to them.

**[05:45:24.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004804)  
So, nandyan nga ang JobStart, meron pong fieldjobs.net, at ang nabanggit po ni Secretary Tolentino kanina is that TESDA put together the skills passport

**[05:45:36.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004805)  
precisely to provide a digital platform to help create these linkages and address information asymmetry para at least hindi po information ang kulang.

**[05:45:48.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004806)  
So that JobStart, fieldjobs.net, and so on.

**[05:45:53.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004807)  
Our...

**[05:45:54.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004808)  
At the least, TVET graduates can have access online in real time to the job openings that are available.

**[05:46:03.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004809)  
These are all being designed to make sure that our supply and our demand meet together.

**[05:46:10.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004810)  
So, ang ano na lang dyan, ma'am, is how effectively, how intensively, no, both Dole and TESDA can in fact roll out this program with the kind of urgency that we need.

**[05:46:23.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004811)  
Sana naman po.

**[05:46:24.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004814)  
The other piece that should be clear is that we need to make sure that demand side is commensurate.

**[05:46:34.920] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004815)  
So, Bureau of Investment naman po yan because we need to make sure that industry opens up and has as much job openings that are of decent work

**[05:46:43.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004816)  
and of the decent scale of quality of salaries and so on, that again, bahagi po yan sa macroeconomic slowdown ng...

**[05:46:54.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004817)  
ng country.

**[05:46:55.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004818)  
So, kanina, nagtanong po si kong Robert tungkol po sa area-based demand-driven approach ng prioritization ng TESDA.

**[05:47:05.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004819)  
No, what we try to do is to prioritize at least government funds to those sectors that have the most demand or most emerging job openings.

**[05:47:15.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004820)  
So, nandyan po, kagaya ng kanina pang pinag-uusapan ng ICT, ang AI, nandyan po ang actually maritime tourism and services.

**[05:47:24.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004821)  
No, industry.

**[05:47:26.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004822)  
Nandyan po ang semiconductors and advanced manufacturing.

**[05:47:30.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004823)  
All of these are sectors that are being prioritized because we know that the jobs are actually available

**[05:47:37.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-004824)  
and we need to make sure that our workforce is appropriately skilled for the jobs that actually exist.

**[05:47:45.690] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004825)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:47:47.450] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004826)  
A while ago, I was browsing through the website of the DBM and isa po doon sa nakita ko,

**[05:47:54.410] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004827)  
yung 2027.

**[05:47:55.790] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004828)  
budget priorities nila.

**[05:47:57.950] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004829)  
Number one pa rin is human capital development.

**[05:48:01.350] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004830)  
And nakalagay po doon, foster the dynamic, inclusive, and responsive labor market while addressing digital upskilling and reskilling initiatives under the Trabajo para sa Bayan Plan 2025-2034.

**[05:48:17.270] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004831)  
Exactly po, magkano ang ibinigay ng DBM po sa inyo for this?

**[05:48:24.610] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004832)  
For this year po, 3 million pesos.

**[05:48:27.610] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004833)  
So, ito po yung budget po para to implement the Trabajo para sa Bayan Plan.

**[05:48:37.270] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004834)  
Yung development po ng plan is it's a joint venture of DOL, DTI, and DepDev.

**[05:48:44.970] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004835)  
At ngayon po ay we're rolling out the plan together with DepDev.

**[05:48:49.430] DOLE Undersecretary Carmela I. Torres** (asr-004836)  
But for this year, we receive 3 million pesos.

**[05:48:55.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004837)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:48:56.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004838)  
Kaya ako po naitanong yan.

**[05:48:58.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004839)  
Kasi hindi ko na po alam kung maniniwala ba ako dito.

**[05:49:01.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004840)  
Kasi nakalagay din dito sa human capital development yung aral ng DepEd.

**[05:49:07.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004841)  
Pero wala din pong pondo yung aral.

**[05:49:09.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004842)  
So, I think this is a concern that I have to raise with DBM.

**[05:49:14.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004843)  
Gusto ko lang pong malaman sa inyo first hand kung talaga bang seryoso po ito kaya ako po naitanong.

**[05:49:21.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004844)  
Anyway po, I still have a lot of questions to ask.

**[05:49:25.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004845)  
Pero hindi ko na po itatanong.

**[05:49:27.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004846)  
Let me just siguro po manifest that naiintindihan ko po ang kalagayan natin.

**[05:49:34.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004847)  
Ang challenge na meron sa atin sa panahon ngayon lalo po sa inyo sa DOLE at sa TESDA.

**[05:49:40.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004848)  
Kaya po, nandiyan dyan po yung unemployment na tumataas.

**[05:49:45.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004849)  
Couple that, add to that yung global uncertainties.

**[05:49:48.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004850)  
And of course, the urgent need to protect our workers both here and abroad.

**[05:49:53.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004851)  
Kaya kami po sa kababaihan, kabalikatan.

**[05:49:56.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004852)  
Para sa kapakanan at kaunlaran ng bayan or 4K, ay nag-i-extend po sa inyo ng wholehearted support.

**[05:50:05.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004853)  
Para maipagpatuloy po ninyo ang vital mission ninyo to safeguard labor rights and creating opportunities for our people.

**[05:50:14.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004854)  
Especially po sa ating mga kababaihan.

**[05:50:18.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004855)  
So, lalo na po ngayon, Mr. Chair, tapusin ko na lamang po itong Mexico Manifestation.

**[05:50:25.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004856)  
At the same time, yung aming pong support comes with it a call that every peso entrusted to DOLE must be spent wisely, transparently, and strategically.

**[05:50:35.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004857)  
The Filipino workforce is our nation's greatest asset and our most valuable export.

**[05:50:41.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004858)  
Kaya tinihiling po natin sa DOLE na lalo pang pagbutihin ang kanilang mga programa upang ang ating mga manggagawa ay maging mas handa,

**[05:50:50.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004859)  
mas protectado, at maging mas competitive po.

**[05:50:55.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004860)  
Sa harap ng pandaigdigang hamon.

**[05:50:57.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004861)  
In these difficult times, we stand with DOLE, but we also challenge it to do better, to rise higher,

**[05:51:03.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004862)  
and to ensure that the 2027 budget truly translates into jobs, dignity, and hope for millions of Filipino workers.

**[05:51:11.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004863)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair. Maraming salamat po, Secretary Tolentino, Secretary Benitez, and to the whole DOLE family.

**[05:51:18.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004864)  
Thank you very much, Rep. Iris Montes.

**[05:51:23.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004865)

**[05:51:58.800] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004871)

**[05:52:03.160] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004872)

**[05:52:08.120] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004873)  
Mr. Chair, may I make a brief manifestation regarding the strict enforcement of our occupational safety and health standards,

**[05:52:19.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004874)

**[05:52:22.580] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004875)  
Particularly contained in the DOLE.

**[05:52:25.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004876)  
Department Order No. 13, Series of 1998.

**[05:52:29.880] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004877)  
Naalala mo, Secretary Tol, you were then Mayor of Kapanon.

**[05:52:35.380] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004878)  
I was the Regional Director of Saudi Tagalog during that time.

**[05:52:39.940] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004879)  
Nung in-issue tong Department Order, pinaresearch ko, hindi na babago po ito eh.

**[05:52:46.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004880)  
28 years ago.

**[05:52:47.620] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004881)  
At sana, there are instances that work-related accidents happen in some ongoing...

**[05:52:55.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004882)  
construction works, which I think deserve our serious attention, Secretary.

**[05:53:01.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004883)  
Mr. Chair, I take note on the observation of the Good Secretary that DOLE must undertake thorough inspection

**[05:53:11.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004884)  
to ensure the compliance to this occupational safety and health standards.

**[05:53:16.460] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004885)

**[05:53:23.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004886)  
saka yung health standards.

**[05:53:25.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004887)  
Karaming aksidente.

**[05:53:26.440] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004888)  
Sa katunayan, may dala akong records dito.

**[05:53:28.520] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004889)  
Mga aksidente nangyayari, sayang mga buhay na mga nawala at saka yung mga aksidente na hindi na pwedeng magtrabaho ngayon.

**[05:53:37.600] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004890)

**[05:53:41.980] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004891)

Pero kahit mapuno po yung 1,210, hindi po sapat na tugunan, lalong-lalo na sa Region 3 na magkaroon ng inspeksyon.

**[05:54:13.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004896)  
Sa Region 3 po, sinabi ko kanina na merong 7,000 establishments na dapat inspeksyonin ng 38 na katao.

Pero sa pananaw ko po ngayon, baka po bawal kasi yung inspector, yung katungkulan na mag-inspeksyon ayon sa Article 128, yung visitorial power ay delegated po ng batas sa akin. Hindi na po pwedeng i-delegate pa uli yun sa dalawang suson. Delegated powers can no longer be delegated. So pwede po sila mag-observe kung tama po yung ginagawa ng mga

inspectors.

**[05:55:27.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-004912)

**[06:15:19.120] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005490)

**[06:15:54.460] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005493)

**[06:16:06.160] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005494)

**[06:16:19.120] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005495)

**[06:16:30.420] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005496)

**[06:16:39.800] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-005497)

under the convergence program on livelihood.

**[06:31:27.210] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005871)

**[06:31:31.710] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005872)

**[06:31:31.790] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005873)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair and Mr. Secretary, Director General, and the DoLE family.

**[06:31:32.430] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005874)  
So actually, I'm here today to help this...

**[06:31:34.750] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005875)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair and Mr. Secretary, Director General, and the DoLE family.

**[06:31:35.470] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005876)  
To help this committee give the department and the whole DoLE family what it needs to effectively perform its mandate under the labor code.

**[06:31:44.050] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005877)  
So, my first question is really about the visitorial and enforcement powers of the DoLE.

**[06:31:51.330] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005878)  
Because as a labor law practitioner before entering politics, I saw that there are some cases wherein if we just fully fund or support or give enough funding for the enforcement power of the DoLE,

**[06:32:05.970] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005881)  
may mga cases po sana na

**[06:32:07.830] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005882)  
natulungan po talaga natin fully yung mga laborers.

**[06:32:10.590] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005883)  
And one of those cases is

**[06:32:12.110] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005884)  
the Manggagawa sa Komunikasyon ng Pilipinas

**[06:32:13.650] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005885)  
versus PLDT,

**[06:32:17.690] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005886)  
which was decided in February 2024.

**[06:32:21.010] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005887)  
So, what happened in 2016 was the Department of Labor and Employment inspected the company pursuant to its Article 128 power.

**[06:32:30.930] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005888)  
And in January 2018, the Secretary of Labor ordered the regularization of the Inspector General's license,

**[06:32:31.330] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005889)  
And in January 2018, the Secretary of Labor ordered the regularization of the contracted

**[06:32:36.970] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005890)  
workers.

**[06:32:37.970] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005891)  
So there was about 7,344 of them on the final count.

**[06:32:42.310] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005892)  
And I think this was one of the largest regularizations that happened in our country.

**[06:32:47.690] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005893)  
So the court set aside some of the categories.

**[06:32:56.030] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005894)  
So they regularized some, but they also set aside some of the categories such as janitorial,

**[06:33:01.910] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005895)  
IT, back office, call centers, and the like.

**[06:33:05.930] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005896)  
So these were thousands of workers, not because the DOLE was wrong, but because the labor

**[06:33:10.650] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005897)  
law compliance officers had interviewed, in the court's words, not more than 1,000 individuals

**[06:33:17.130] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005898)  
and applied the findings to 7,344.

**[06:33:21.130] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005899)  
So the court wrote that conclusions based on sampling or probability in its decision

**[06:33:25.970] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005900)  
should not be considered substantial evidence.

**[06:33:29.610] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005901)  
So my question is, today, how many inspectors are actually on paper, and how many of the

**[06:33:35.250] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005902)  
inspectors are actually on the field deployed and doing their inspection powers?

**[06:33:39.470] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005903)  
A while ago, Your Honor, we were really confronted with that continuing challenge of lack of

**[06:33:48.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005904)  
manpower insofar as the labor inspectorate is concerned.

**[06:33:52.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005905)  
Yung inabutan ko, dapat daw ay 1,210

**[06:33:56.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005906)  
ang sa buong Pilipinas,

**[06:33:57.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005907)  
subalit sa ngayon ay wala pang 700.

**[06:34:02.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005908)  
Dumaan sila sa isang masusing eksaminasyon, subalit kahit

**[06:34:09.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005909)  
mabuo yung 1,210, that would not be sufficient

**[06:34:15.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005910)  
to address that continuing concern.

**[06:34:16.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005911)  
Kulang talaga. Hindi kaya, hindi lamang sa mga pribadong establishments

**[06:34:25.800] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005912)  
kundi pati government projects.

**[06:34:27.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005913)  
Ang nag-i-inspect din po noon, DOLE.

**[06:34:28.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005914)  
So hindi talaga kayang punuan o

**[06:34:30.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005915)  
So hindi talaga kayang

**[06:34:32.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005916)  
punuan o gampanan. So as to the details of, I agree with you,

**[06:34:38.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005917)  
yung kaso sigurong iyon ay kung nagkaroon ng tama at

**[06:34:41.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005918)  
appropriate labor inspection,

**[06:34:45.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005919)  
Article 128

**[06:34:46.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005920)  
ay siguro

**[06:34:47.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005921)  
may

**[06:34:48.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005922)  
sapat na

**[06:34:51.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005925)  
compliance

**[06:34:52.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005926)  
kasi na-correct

**[06:34:53.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005927)  
naman po iyan eh.

**[06:34:54.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005928)  
But as to the

**[06:34:55.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005929)  
other details,

**[06:34:56.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005930)  
is BLE here

**[06:34:57.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005931)  
o sino

**[06:34:57.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005932)  
sasagot?

**[06:34:58.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005933)  
Yes.

**[06:34:59.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005934)  
Usec Legargo, would you like to contribute something with permission of the Chair?

**[06:35:03.420] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005935)  
With the kind permission of the Honorable Secretary, the Honorable Members of the Committee,

**[06:35:11.600] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005936)  
Congressman, ibe-verify pa namin sa Court of Appeals and Supreme Court

**[06:35:19.520] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005937)  
because it has already been decided, and then we will give you the final outcome.

**[06:35:27.000] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005938)

**[06:35:36.860] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005939)

**[06:35:41.860] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005940)  
Thank you.

**[06:35:43.400] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005941)  
Sa bagong Department Order No. 238.

**[06:35:50.420] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005942)  
Thank you.

**[06:35:52.500] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005943)  
Thank you, Usec and Mr. Secretary.

**[06:35:55.640] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005944)  
So, right now,

**[06:35:56.720] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005945)

**[06:36:02.720] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005946)

**[06:36:07.480] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005947)

**[06:36:28.100] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005951)

**[06:36:30.920] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005952)

**[06:36:39.140] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005953)

**[06:36:44.820] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005955)

**[06:36:46.540] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005956)

**[06:36:56.820] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005958)

**[06:36:59.700] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005959)

**[06:37:26.960] DOLE Undersecretary Felipe N. Egargo Jr.** (asr-005961)

Actually, I ask these questions po just to emphasize how important it is to support and to fully fund

**[06:37:43.030] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005963)

**[06:37:49.150] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005965)

**[06:37:55.130] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005966)

**[06:37:59.170] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005967)

**[06:38:05.450] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005968)

**[06:38:09.650] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005969)

**[06:38:14.770] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005973)

**[06:38:19.570] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005974)

**[06:38:23.650] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005975)

**[06:38:27.870] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005976)  
So yung question ko lang po, yung Region 5 po, RTWPB,

**[06:38:32.010] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005977)

**[06:38:51.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005979)

**[06:38:53.300] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-005980)

With the permission po of our honorable Secretary, the Bicol Region, in consideration of the current

**[06:39:23.870] DOLE Region V Regional Director Geraldine M. Panlilio** (asr-005982)  
wage order na ₱455 po ang current minimum wage. Currently po, undergoing na kami, diretso na, mayroon na pong kino-conduct na wage deliberation para po ma-review na natin talagang kailangan na

**[06:39:42.620] DOLE Region V Regional Director Geraldine M. Panlilio** (asr-005983)  
because of the current situation. Thank you po.

**[06:39:47.420] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005983-split-2)

**[06:39:50.910] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-005984)  
regarding that so what does the wage commission

**[07:29:55.950] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007200)  
at sa lahat na po. Yun lang po

**[07:30:00.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007212)  
Your Honor, iyon lang po iyong pagbisita ko na siguro na-capture lang sa social media.

**[07:30:06.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007213)  
Mahalaga ito, Mr. Chair, dahil nakita natin na wala akong national government agency

**[07:30:11.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007214)  
ang sumisilip na kumusta na ba yung ating mga public markets.

**[07:30:16.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007215)  
Kumusta na ba yung interest ng market vendors?

**[07:30:18.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007216)  
Tulad ng nasabi ninyo, marami nang nagiging privado o may mga PPP sa mga market natin sa buong bansa.

**[07:30:25.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007217)  
Paano natin matitiyak na may bosses pa rin sila pagdating sa palisya

**[07:30:28.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007218)  
na kung may mga issues at concerns sila sa mga PPP ay nagagawan ng aksyon?

**[07:30:34.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007219)  
Kaya tayo ay mag-propose ng isang dialogue or consultation sa DOLE tungkol dito.

**[07:30:41.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007220)  
Bilang panghuli, Mr. Chair, tungkol sa COA report, tungkol sa repeat beneficiary sa TUPAD.

**[07:30:48.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007221)  
May ginawa na bang aksyon dito ang DOLE?

**[07:30:52.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007222)  
Mahalaga ito sa kapulungan na ito dahil billion-billion ang nakalaan dito sa TUPAD.

**[07:30:57.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007223)  
Yung dapat sanang billion-billion para sa trabaho at kabuhayan, dito ho nakalaan.

**[07:31:03.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007224)  
Kaya dapat maiwasan natin yung ganitong mga gawi na may repeat beneficiaries

**[07:31:07.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007225)  
habang nananawagan pa rin tayo na dapat sana billion-billion din yung pondo para sa domestic job creation.

**[07:31:15.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007226)  
Ayun na po yung panghuli kong katanungan at manifestation, Mr. Chair.

**[07:31:19.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007227)  
Maraming salamat sa DOLE at papakinggan ko ang inyong aksyon tungkol sa bagay na ito.

**[07:31:24.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007228)  
Siguro po si Asec Ami Reyes can provide.

**[07:31:27.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007229)  
Patungkol doon sa umuulit kasi dapat may grace period yun.

**[07:31:34.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007230)  
We avoid favoritism. We give others the job opportunities as well.

**[07:31:41.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007231)  
Thank you, Sec.

**[07:31:43.800] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007232)  
Regarding po doon sa mga complaints na may mga like double availments, yung po ba ang ibig niyo pong banggitin?

**[07:31:51.700] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007233)  
Kasi under our existing guidelines, dapat once lang.

**[07:31:57.980] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007234)  
Every calendar year.

**[07:31:59.040] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007235)  
So ngayon, we are enhancing our TUPAD monitoring system na i-implement ng lahat po ng mga regional offices

**[07:32:10.160] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007236)  
para sa ganon makita ka agad kung may duplication.

**[07:32:13.900] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007237)  
And there is already a coordination being made with the PSA on the use of their portal to easily check yung validity po, no?

**[07:32:26.920] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007238)  
At saka yung coordination.

**[07:32:27.980] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007239)  
Correctness and authenticity of the IDs being presented.

**[07:32:33.160] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007240)  
And of course, ito pong recently lang ho, our Secretary issued a memorandum to all our regional offices to closely monitor iyong implementation ho ng TUPAD in all regions and provinces and areas

**[07:32:49.440] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007241)  
to ensure that what is being delivered as part of the performance under the contract of TUPAD,

**[07:32:57.040] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007242)  
are being done based on the terms of their hiring and based on the work program.

**[07:33:05.880] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007243)  
Meron po kasing work program, ma'am, yun bago ho ma-approve yung sa 2-pad natin.

**[07:33:12.360] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007244)  
So nandiyan po kami sa punto na kailangan talaga close monitoring.

**[07:33:17.940] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007245)  
Challenge din po sa amin yan kasi we have also very limited number of our personnel on the ground.

**[07:33:25.540] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007246)  
And tumutulong po.

**[07:33:27.040] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007247)  
And if we have a partner agency or partner government under our convergence program, we also ask them to help in monitoring.

**[07:33:39.420] DOLE Assistant Secretary Amuerfina R. Reyes** (asr-007248)  
So it's not just about fielding them on the ground, but seeing to it that they are doing their job and they are also protected while they are doing their job.

**[07:33:49.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007249)  
Maraming salamat.

**[07:33:49.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007250)  
Alagay ko na lang ho sa record ang ating request para sa status report ng inyong...

**[07:33:57.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007251)  
programa para sa paggamit ng single entry approach at ang compliance ng DOLE at ng TESDA sa kanyang gender and development mandate.

**[07:34:06.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007252)  
Maraming salamat.

**[07:34:09.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-007253)  
Thank you very much, Representative Sara.

**[07:34:12.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-007254)  
Next to give his manifestation, let us recognize Representative Miro Quimbo.

**[07:34:20.520] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007255)  
Thank you very much.

**[07:34:21.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007256)  
Anyway, we're waiting for Chairman Mika.

**[07:34:25.900] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007257)  
I just...

**[07:34:26.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007258)  
While it is a manifestation, can I ask Attorney Roy Buenafe to please take the seat?

**[07:34:33.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007259)  
I just have a very quick...

**[07:34:35.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007260)  
Because I don't want us leaving with a misimpression regarding wage order number 27 and 28.

**[07:34:43.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007261)  
So clarify ko lang ha.

**[07:34:45.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007262)  
So wage order number 26, 18 July 2025, the minimum wage was 695 pesos, correct?

**[07:34:55.180] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007263)  
With the permission of...

**[07:34:56.500] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007264)  
of the Secretary, Mr. Chair, that is correct po.

**[07:34:59.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007265)  
Wage order number 27, one year later, issued on 20 July 2026, mandated 755 pesos, correct?

**[07:35:08.760] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007266)  
Yes po, on the first tranche, Mr. Chair.

**[07:35:10.760] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007267)  
Correct.

**[07:35:11.300] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007268)  
60 pesos on the first tranche.

**[07:35:13.260] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007269)  
Yes po.

**[07:35:13.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007270)  
20 pesos come January 26.

**[07:35:16.360] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007271)  
25 pesos po.

**[07:35:17.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007272)  
25 pesos come January 2027, correct?

**[07:35:21.740] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007273)  
Tama po.

**[07:35:22.120] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007274)  
But that Wage Order No. 27 has been sent to purgatory.

**[07:35:26.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007275)  
By virtue of the injunction application, which effectively was granted, but is still awaiting resolution relative to the failure or non-posting of the bond, correct?

**[07:35:39.080] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007276)  
Tama po, Mr. Chair.

**[07:35:39.980] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007277)  
But the court has not yet set it aside.

**[07:35:43.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007278)  
So, in short, the injunction remains effective until and unless the court issues an order rescinding the same.

**[07:35:52.280] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007279)  
Tama?

**[07:35:53.100] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007280)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[07:35:54.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007281)  
So, while in fact there has been...

**[07:35:56.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007282)  
On record, no filing of any bond, we cannot yet assume that the injunction is found to be sufficient until and unless the court orders so.

**[07:36:07.920] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007283)  
As a lawyer also, Mr. Chair, I believe so.

**[07:36:09.940] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007284)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[07:36:10.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007285)  
Correct.

**[07:36:11.180] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007286)  
Tama?

**[07:36:12.020] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007287)  
Tama po.

**[07:36:12.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007288)  
Okay.

**[07:36:13.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007289)  
So, in view of that, and in view of the seeming difficulties that our workers are going through,

**[07:36:21.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007290)  
the Wage Board took the initiative and exercised its extraordinary powers, recognizing the difficulty that the workers are going through despite the quagmire of this injunction. You said, let us issue No. 28 para ma-implement natin at ma-address natin ang pangangailangan ng mga manggagawa.

**[07:36:42.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007317)  
Tama ba?

**[07:36:42.820] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007318)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[07:36:44.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007319)  
That's really the rationality behind Wage Order No. 28.

**[07:36:49.200] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007320)  
Yes or no lang para mabilis tayo, ha?

**[07:36:50.380] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007321)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[07:36:51.380] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007322)  
Yes po.

**[07:36:52.220] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007323)  
So, effectively, it is incorrect to say that we just...

**[07:36:56.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007324)  
order number 28 is a

**[07:36:58.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007325)  
reduction. It cannot be

**[07:37:00.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007326)  
a reduction because wage order number

**[07:37:02.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007327)  
27 never took life.

**[07:37:05.260] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007328)  
So as far as we are concerned

**[07:37:06.660] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007329)  
today, it's wage order number

**[07:37:08.380] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007330)  
26 at 695

**[07:37:10.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007331)  
pesos. So wage order number

**[07:37:12.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007332)  
28 is increasing

**[07:37:14.720] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-007333)  
it by 60 pesos. Correct?

**[07:37:16.940] DOLE NCR Regional Director Atty. Roy L. Buenafe** (asr-007334)

**[08:03:28.750] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007772)

**[08:03:57.690] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007776)

**[08:04:09.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007777)

**[08:04:12.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007778)

**[08:04:17.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007779)

**[08:04:27.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007781)

**[08:04:33.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007782)

**[08:04:38.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007783)

**[08:04:46.820] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007784)  
to be sustained,

**[08:04:47.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007785)

**[08:04:57.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007786)

**[08:05:04.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007787)

**[08:05:08.480] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007788)

**[08:05:29.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007791)

**[08:05:32.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007792)

**[08:05:42.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007793)

Subalit kanina mayroon din akong request for

**[08:06:14.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-007799)
